B|000001|PACKAGE BODY         "PKG_DMF0020"{LF}|#
B|000002|AS{LF}|#
B|000003|PROCEDURE P_DMF0020_GET_MEASURE_COUNTINFO_SAVE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|000004|({LF}|#
B|000005|/* 보류 PCI 협의 3/19{LF}|#
B|000006|    P_USER_ID         IN VARCHAR2, -- For EditYn{LF}|#
B|000007|    P_MNU_ID          IN VARCHAR2, -- For EditYn{LF}|#
B|000008|*/{LF}|#
B|000009|    O_RC1             OUT SYS_REFCURSOR,{LF}|#
B|000010|    O_RESULT_CURSOR   OUT SYS_REFCURSOR{LF}|#
B|000011|){LF}|#
B|000012|/*{LF}|#
B|000013| * Program ID   : DMF0020_GET_MEASURE_COUNTINFO{LF}|#
B|000014| * Description  :{LF}|#
B|000015| * Program History{LF}|#
B|000016| *==================================================================================================={LF}|#
B|000017| *   Date       In Charge      Description{LF}|#
B|000018| *==================================================================================================={LF}|#
B|000019| * 2021/03/04  kuniid         [C20210127-000163] GSCM Sales Price 일괄입력 기능 개선 요청{LF}|#
B|000020| * 2025.01.13{TAB}KJJ{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}[PJ2024-50611] Sales Price 로직 수정 - 별도 룩업이 아니라 해당화면의 메저에서 가져오게 변경{LF}|#
B|000021| * 2026.03.16  jcnam          [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발{LF}|#
B|000022| */{LF}|#
B|000023|AS{LF}|#
B|000024|    V_PROC_INPUT     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000025|    V_BACKTRACE      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000026|    V_SQL_CODE       VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000027|    V_SQL_ERRMSG     VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000028|    V_LOG_TYPE       VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000029|    V_LOG_MESSAGE    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000030|    V_DESCRIPTION    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000031|    N_LOG_COUNT      PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#
B|000032|    D_EVENT_DATE     DATE        := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000033|BEGIN{LF}|#
B|000034|    OPEN O_RC1 FOR{LF}|#
B|000035|    SELECT DISTINCT T1.COLUMN_LABEL_NAME AS LOOKUP_CODE{LF}|#
B|000036|         , T1.COLUMN_LABEL_NAME AS DESCRIPTION{LF}|#
B|000037|         , 99                AS DISPLAY_ALL{LF}|#
B|000038|         -- ,NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|000039|    FROM   SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE T1{LF}|#
B|000040|    /* 보류 PCI 협의 3/19{LF}|#
B|000041|        INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|000042|                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V{LF}|#
B|000043|                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID{LF}|#
B|000044|                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|000045|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#
B|000046|     ON T2.UNIT_ID in ({LF}|#
B|000047|         SELECT TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(T1.DIVISION_ID, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL)){LF}|#
B|000048|         FROM   DUAL{LF}|#

B|000375|    SELECT DISTINCT T1.YYYYMMDD, SUBSTR(T1.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) AS YYYYMM, T1.DIVISION_ID, T1.CURRENCY, T1.UNIT_CURRENCY, T1.EXCHANGE_PRICE, NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|000376|    FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE T1{LF}|#
B|000377|                INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|000378|                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V{LF}|#
B|000379|                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID{LF}|#
B|000380|                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|000381|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#
B|000382|            ON T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID{LF}|#
B|000383|    WHERE{LF}|#
B|000384|    T1.YYYYMMDD >= P_START_YYYYMMDD AND T1.YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|000385|    AND    SUBSTR(T1.YYYYMMDD, 5, 2) BETWEEN '01' AND '12'; -- 2026.08.31 AJS{LF}|#
B|000386|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000387|    -- 조회 성공 O_RESULT_CURSOR (Case 3 표준 패턴){LF}|#
B|000388|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000389|    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|000390|    SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|000391|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000392|    -- END 로그{LF}|#
B|000393|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000394|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|000395|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|000396|    V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|000397|    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000398|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000399|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|000400|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|000401|        ROLLBACK;{LF}|#
B|000402|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|000403|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#
B|000404|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|000405|        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000406|        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);{LF}|#
B|000407|        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;{LF}|#
B|000408|        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT({LF}|#
B|000409|            'P_START_YYYYMMDD' VALUE P_START_YYYYMMDD,{LF}|#
B|000410|            'P_END_YYYYMMDD'   VALUE P_END_YYYYMMDD,{LF}|#
B|000411|            'P_USER_ID'   VALUE P_USER_ID,{LF}|#
B|000412|            'P_MNU_ID'   VALUE P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|000413|        );{LF}|#
B|000414|        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;{LF}|#
B|000415|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|000416|            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH({LF}|#
B|000417|                $$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE,{LF}|#
B|000418|                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|000419|                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|000420|            );{LF}|#
B|000421|        END;{LF}|#
B|000422|        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,{LF}|#
B|000423|          '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||{LF}|#
B|000424|          ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||{LF}|#
B|000425|          ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||{LF}|#
B|000426|          ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT{LF}|#
B|000427|        );{LF}|#
B|000428|        RAISE;{LF}|#
B|000429|END P_DMF0020_GET_FUTUREPRICE_INFO;{LF}|#
B|000430|PROCEDURE P_DMF0000_SAVE_EXCHANGE_POPUP_DATA{LF}|#

B|000435|    , P_CURRENCY               IN VARCHAR2{LF}|#
B|000436|    , P_UNIT_CURRENCY          IN VARCHAR2{LF}|#
B|000437|    , P_EXCHANGE_PRICE         IN VARCHAR2{LF}|#
B|000438|    , P_CREATED_BY             IN VARCHAR2{LF}|#
B|000439|    , P_LAST_UPDATED_BY        IN VARCHAR2{LF}|#

B|000452| * {TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}DMF0042_SAVE_DM_BP_EXCHANGE_RATE 참고 해서 만듬{LF}|#
B|000453| * 2026.03.16  jcnam            [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발{LF}|#

B|000455|    -- EXCEPTION 블록 필수 변수{LF}|#

B|000463|    N_LOG_COUNT     PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#

B|000474|    V_EXCHANGE_PRICE    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000475|    V_CREATED_BY        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000476|    V_LAST_UPDATED_BY   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000477|    V_DELIMITER         VARCHAR2(1 CHAR) := '^';{LF}|#
B|000478|    -- 파싱용 로컬 복사본 (IN 파라미터는 수정 불가하므로){LF}|#

B|000485|    V_P_CREATED_BY      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000486|    V_P_LAST_UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|000487|    V_IDX_AF            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000488|    V_IDX_DI            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000489|    V_IDX_YM            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000490|    V_IDX_CR            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000491|    V_IDX_UC            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000492|    V_IDX_EP            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000493|    V_IDX_CB            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000494|    V_IDX_LU            PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|000495|    N_DELETE_COUNT      PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#

B|000498|    V_RC1               SYS_REFCURSOR;{LF}|#
B|000499|BEGIN{LF}|#
B|000500|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000501|    -- GTT 초기화{LF}|#
B|000502|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000503|    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS;{LF}|#
B|000504|    -- EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION SET GLOBAL_TEMP_TABLE_STATS = SESSION';{LF}|#
B|000505|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#

B|000507|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#

B|000509|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## Start of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|000510|    V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|000511|    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000512|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000513|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000514|    -- recv init{LF}|#
B|000515|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000516|    V_RECV_ACTIVE_FLAG := P_ACTIVE_FLAG;{LF}|#
B|000517|    V_ACTIVE_FLAG     := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000518|    V_DIVISION_ID     := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000519|    V_YYYYMMDD        := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000520|    V_CURRENCY        := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000521|    V_UNIT_CURRENCY   := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000522|    V_EXCHANGE_PRICE  := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000523|    V_CREATED_BY      := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000524|    V_LAST_UPDATED_BY := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000525|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000526|    -- 마지막 데이터 처리를 위한 구분자 추가{LF}|#
B|000527|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000528|    V_P_ACTIVE_FLAG     := RTRIM(P_ACTIVE_FLAG)     || '^';{LF}|#
B|000529|    V_P_DIVISION_ID     := RTRIM(P_DIVISION_ID)     || '^';{LF}|#
B|000530|    V_P_YYYYMMDD        := RTRIM(P_YYYYMMDD)        || '^';{LF}|#
B|000531|    V_P_CURRENCY        := RTRIM(P_CURRENCY)        || '^';{LF}|#
B|000532|    V_P_UNIT_CURRENCY   := RTRIM(P_UNIT_CURRENCY)   || '^';{LF}|#
B|000533|    V_P_EXCHANGE_PRICE  := RTRIM(P_EXCHANGE_PRICE)  || '^';{LF}|#
B|000534|    V_P_CREATED_BY      := RTRIM(P_CREATED_BY)      || '^';{LF}|#
B|000535|    V_P_LAST_UPDATED_BY := RTRIM(P_LAST_UPDATED_BY) || '^';{LF}|#
B|000536|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000537|    -- GT_D000_RESULTS 테이블에 split후 insert (START){LF}|#
B|000538|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000539|    WHILE LENGTH(V_P_DIVISION_ID) > 0{LF}|#
B|000540|    LOOP{LF}|#
B|000541|        V_IDX_AF := INSTR(V_P_ACTIVE_FLAG,     '^');{LF}|#
B|000542|        V_IDX_DI := INSTR(V_P_DIVISION_ID,     '^');{LF}|#

B|000546|        V_IDX_EP := INSTR(V_P_EXCHANGE_PRICE,  '^');{LF}|#
B|000547|        V_IDX_CB := INSTR(V_P_CREATED_BY,      '^');{LF}|#
B|000548|        V_IDX_LU := INSTR(V_P_LAST_UPDATED_BY, '^');{LF}|#
B|000549|        IF V_IDX_DI > 0 THEN{LF}|#
B|000550|            V_ACTIVE_FLAG     := SUBSTR(V_P_ACTIVE_FLAG,     1, V_IDX_AF - 1);{LF}|#
B|000551|            V_P_ACTIVE_FLAG   := SUBSTR(V_P_ACTIVE_FLAG,     V_IDX_AF + 1);{LF}|#
B|000552|            V_DIVISION_ID     := SUBSTR(V_P_DIVISION_ID,     1, V_IDX_DI - 1);{LF}|#
B|000553|            V_P_DIVISION_ID   := SUBSTR(V_P_DIVISION_ID,     V_IDX_DI + 1);{LF}|#
B|000554|            V_YYYYMMDD        := SUBSTR(V_P_YYYYMMDD,        1, V_IDX_YM - 1);{LF}|#
B|000555|            V_P_YYYYMMDD      := SUBSTR(V_P_YYYYMMDD,        V_IDX_YM + 1);{LF}|#
B|000556|            V_CURRENCY        := SUBSTR(V_P_CURRENCY,        1, V_IDX_CR - 1);{LF}|#
B|000557|            V_P_CURRENCY      := SUBSTR(V_P_CURRENCY,        V_IDX_CR + 1);{LF}|#
B|000558|            V_UNIT_CURRENCY   := SUBSTR(V_P_UNIT_CURRENCY,   1, V_IDX_UC - 1);{LF}|#
B|000559|            V_P_UNIT_CURRENCY := SUBSTR(V_P_UNIT_CURRENCY,   V_IDX_UC + 1);{LF}|#
B|000560|            V_EXCHANGE_PRICE  := SUBSTR(V_P_EXCHANGE_PRICE,  1, V_IDX_EP - 1);{LF}|#
B|000561|            V_P_EXCHANGE_PRICE:= SUBSTR(V_P_EXCHANGE_PRICE,  V_IDX_EP + 1);{LF}|#
B|000562|            V_CREATED_BY      := SUBSTR(V_P_CREATED_BY,      1, V_IDX_CB - 1);{LF}|#
B|000563|            V_P_CREATED_BY    := SUBSTR(V_P_CREATED_BY,      V_IDX_CB + 1);{LF}|#
B|000564|            V_LAST_UPDATED_BY := SUBSTR(V_P_LAST_UPDATED_BY, 1, V_IDX_LU - 1);{LF}|#
B|000565|            V_P_LAST_UPDATED_BY := SUBSTR(V_P_LAST_UPDATED_BY, V_IDX_LU + 1);{LF}|#
B|000566|            --■■■■■■■■■■ 40 Validation 필요 대상 : UNIT_CURRENCY 유무{LF}|#
B|000567|            IF V_UNIT_CURRENCY IS NULL OR V_UNIT_CURRENCY = '' THEN{LF}|#
B|000568|                V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|000569|                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|000570|                V_DESCRIPTION := 'Unit이 입력되어있지 않습니다.' || CHR(10) || CHR(13) || 'LOOKUP : CURRENCY_LIST 에서 통화를 등록해주세요.';{LF}|#
B|000571|                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000572|                BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000573|                OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|000574|                SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: - Validation Check' AS MSG, V_DESCRIPTION AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|000575|                RETURN;{LF}|#
B|000576|            END IF;{LF}|#
B|000577|            --■■■■■■■■■■ 40 Validation 필요 대상 : EXCHANGE_PRICE는 0보다 커야 한다. (음수거나 0이면 안됨){LF}|#
B|000578|            IF NVL(TO_NUMBER(V_EXCHANGE_PRICE), 0) <= 0 THEN{LF}|#
B|000579|                V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|000580|                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|000581|                V_DESCRIPTION := '0 보다 크게 입력 해주세요.';{LF}|#
B|000582|                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000583|                BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000584|                OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|000585|                SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: - Validation Check' AS MSG, V_DESCRIPTION AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|000586|                RETURN;{LF}|#
B|000587|            END IF;{LF}|#
B|000588|            INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS{LF}|#
B|000589|                ( ACTIVE_FLAG, DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY, EXCHANGE_PRICE, CREATED_BY, LAST_UPDATED_BY ){LF}|#

B|000591|                ( V_ACTIVE_FLAG, V_DIVISION_ID, V_YYYYMMDD, V_CURRENCY, V_UNIT_CURRENCY, V_EXCHANGE_PRICE, V_CREATED_BY, V_LAST_UPDATED_BY );{LF}|#
B|000592|        ELSE{LF}|#
B|000593|            V_DIVISION_ID   := V_P_DIVISION_ID;{LF}|#
B|000594|            V_P_DIVISION_ID := NULL;{LF}|#
B|000595|            INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS{LF}|#
B|000596|                ( ACTIVE_FLAG, DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY, EXCHANGE_PRICE, CREATED_BY, LAST_UPDATED_BY ){LF}|#
B|000597|            VALUES{LF}|#
B|000598|                ( V_ACTIVE_FLAG, V_DIVISION_ID, V_YYYYMMDD, V_CURRENCY, V_UNIT_CURRENCY, V_EXCHANGE_PRICE, V_CREATED_BY, V_LAST_UPDATED_BY );{LF}|#
B|000599|        END IF;{LF}|#
B|000600|        -- Division_id Null Validation{LF}|#
B|000601|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|000602|            SELECT 1 INTO V_EXISTS FROM DUAL{LF}|#
B|000603|            WHERE EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|000604|                SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MST_DIVISION WHERE 1 = 1 AND DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|000605|            );{LF}|#
B|000606|        EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|000607|            WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN{LF}|#
B|000608|                V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|000609|                V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#

B|000611|                D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000612|                BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000613|                OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#

B|000615|                RETURN;{LF}|#

B|000617|    END LOOP;{LF}|#

B|000619|    -- GT_D000_RESULTS 테이블에 split후 insert (END){LF}|#

B|000623|    -- GTT 통계 수집{LF}|#
B|000624|    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|000625|        ownname          => 'SCM_MGR',{LF}|#
B|000626|        tabname          => 'GT_D000_RESULTS',{LF}|#
B|000627|        estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE,{LF}|#
B|000628|        method_opt       => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'{LF}|#
B|000629|    );{LF}|#
B|000630|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000631|    --■■■■■■■■■■ Validation Check : 중복된 데이터 확인 (START){LF}|#
B|000632|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000633|    BEGIN{LF}|#
B|000634|        SELECT 1 INTO V_EXISTS FROM DUAL{LF}|#
B|000635|        WHERE EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|000636|            SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|000637|            FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE A{LF}|#
B|000638|            INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS B{LF}|#
B|000639|                    ON A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|000640|                   AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|000641|                   AND A.CURRENCY      = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000642|                   AND A.UNIT_CURRENCY = B.UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000643|            WHERE B.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'I'{LF}|#
B|000644|        );{LF}|#
B|000645|        -- 존재하면 중복{LF}|#
B|000646|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|000647|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|000648|        V_DESCRIPTION := '중복된 데이터가 존재 합니다.';{LF}|#
B|000649|        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000650|        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000651|        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|000652|        SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: - Validation Check' AS MSG, V_DESCRIPTION AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|000653|        RETURN;{LF}|#
B|000654|    EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|000655|        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN{LF}|#
B|000656|            NULL; -- 중복 없음 → 정상 진행{LF}|#
B|000657|    END;{LF}|#
B|000658|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000659|    --■■■■■■■■■■ Validation Check : 중복된 데이터 확인 (END){LF}|#
B|000660|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000661|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000662|    -- DELETE (START){LF}|#
B|000663|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000664|    DELETE{LF}|#
B|000665|    FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE{LF}|#
B|000666|    WHERE EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|000667|        SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS R{LF}|#
B|000668|        WHERE R.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'D'{LF}|#
B|000669|        AND DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.DIVISION_ID   = R.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|000670|        AND DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.YYYYMMDD      = R.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|000671|        AND DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.CURRENCY      = R.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000672|        AND DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.UNIT_CURRENCY = R.UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000673|    );{LF}|#
B|000674|    N_DELETE_COUNT := SQL%ROWCOUNT;{LF}|#
B|000675|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000676|    -- DELETE (END){LF}|#
B|000677|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000678|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000679|    -- UPDATE, INSERT (START){LF}|#
B|000680|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000681|    MERGE INTO DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE A{LF}|#
B|000682|    USING ({LF}|#
B|000683|        SELECT DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000684|             , MAX(EXCHANGE_PRICE)  AS EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|000685|             , MAX(CREATED_BY)      AS CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000686|             , MAX(LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000687|        FROM  SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS{LF}|#
B|000688|        WHERE ACTIVE_FLAG <> 'D'   -- DELETE값이 아닌경우만{LF}|#
B|000689|        GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000690|    ) B{LF}|#
B|000691|        ON (    A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|000692|            AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|000693|            AND A.CURRENCY      = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000694|            AND A.UNIT_CURRENCY = B.UNIT_CURRENCY ){LF}|#
B|000695|    WHEN MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|000696|    UPDATE SET A.EXCHANGE_PRICE   = B.EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|000697|             , A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = B.LAST_UPDATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000698|             , A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = SYSDATE{LF}|#
B|000699|    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|000700|    INSERT ( DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY, EXCHANGE_PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE ){LF}|#
B|000701|    VALUES ( B.DIVISION_ID, B.YYYYMMDD, B.CURRENCY, B.UNIT_CURRENCY, B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, B.CREATED_BY, SYSDATE, B.LAST_UPDATED_BY, SYSDATE );{LF}|#
B|000702|    N_MERGE_COUNT := SQL%ROWCOUNT;{LF}|#
B|000703|    INSERT INTO DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE_H{LF}|#
B|000704|    ( HISTORY_SEQ{LF}|#
B|000705|    , DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY, EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|000706|    , CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE ){LF}|#
B|000707|    SELECT SEQ1_DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.NEXTVAL{LF}|#
B|000708|         , T.DIVISION_ID, T.YYYYMMDD, T.CURRENCY, T.UNIT_CURRENCY, T.EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|000709|         , T.CREATED_BY, SYSDATE AS CREATION_DATE, T.LAST_UPDATED_BY, SYSDATE AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE{LF}|#
B|000710|    FROM ({LF}|#
B|000711|        SELECT DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000712|             , MAX(EXCHANGE_PRICE)  AS EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|000713|             , MAX(CREATED_BY)      AS CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000714|             , MAX(LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000715|        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_D000_RESULTS{LF}|#
B|000716|        WHERE ACTIVE_FLAG <> 'D'{LF}|#
B|000717|        GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|000718|    ) T;{LF}|#
B|000719|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000720|    -- 저장이 성공한 이후에 실행되는 프로시저 (START){LF}|#
B|000721|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000722|    -- 환율이 변경되면, Sales Price, Sales Plan AMT 가 같이 업데이트 되어야 한다.{LF}|#
B|000723|    -- 환율화면에서 환율이 변경되면 아래 2개의 프로시저가  호출되어야 한다.{LF}|#
B|000724|    -- Sales Price Update{LF}|#
B|000725|    P_DMF0020_SAVE_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_CONVERSION(   /* [E20260824-PKGCONSOL] 단독 SP 호출 -> PKG 내부 호출 */ V_DIVISION_ID, V_RC1);{LF}|#
B|000726|    -- Sales Plan Update{LF}|#
B|000727|    /* TO-DO 4/1 전환 예정{LF}|#
B|000728|    SCM_MGR.PR_DM_ANL_SALES_PLAN_AMT_CREATE_LOCAL_KRW(V_DIVISION_ID, 'EXCHANGE_RATE_UI');{LF}|#
B|000729|    */{LF}|#
B|000730|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000731|    -- 저장이 성공한 이후에 실행되는 프로시저 (END){LF}|#
B|000732|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|000733|    -- COMMIT{LF}|#
B|000734|    IF DBMS_TRANSACTION.LOCAL_TRANSACTION_ID IS NOT NULL THEN{LF}|#
B|000735|        COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|000736|    END IF;{LF}|#
B|000737|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000738|    -- DML 성공 O_RESULT_CURSOR (Case 3 표준 패턴){LF}|#
B|000739|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000740|    N_LOG_COUNT := N_DELETE_COUNT + N_MERGE_COUNT;{LF}|#
B|000741|    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|000742|    SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, TO_CHAR(N_LOG_COUNT) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|000743|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000744|    -- END 로그{LF}|#
B|000745|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|000746|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|000747|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|000748|    V_DESCRIPTION := 'delete_count: ' || TO_CHAR(N_DELETE_COUNT) || ' / merge_count: ' || TO_CHAR(N_MERGE_COUNT);{LF}|#
B|000749|    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000750|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|000751|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|000752|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|000753|        ROLLBACK;{LF}|#
B|000754|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|000755|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#
B|000756|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|000757|        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|000758|        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);{LF}|#
B|000759|        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;{LF}|#
B|000760|        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT({LF}|#
B|000761|            'P_ACTIVE_FLAG'     VALUE P_ACTIVE_FLAG,{LF}|#
B|000762|            'P_DIVISION_ID'     VALUE P_DIVISION_ID,{LF}|#
B|000763|            'P_YYYYMMDD'        VALUE P_YYYYMMDD,{LF}|#
B|000764|            'P_CURRENCY'        VALUE P_CURRENCY,{LF}|#

B|000766|            'P_EXCHANGE_PRICE'  VALUE P_EXCHANGE_PRICE,{LF}|#
B|000767|            'P_CREATED_BY'      VALUE P_CREATED_BY,{LF}|#
B|000768|            'P_LAST_UPDATED_BY' VALUE P_LAST_UPDATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000769|        );{LF}|#
B|000770|        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;{LF}|#
B|000771|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|000772|            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH({LF}|#
B|000773|                $$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE,{LF}|#
B|000774|                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|000775|                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|000776|            );{LF}|#
B|000777|        END;{LF}|#
B|000778|        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,{LF}|#
B|000779|          '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||{LF}|#
B|000780|          ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||{LF}|#
B|000781|          ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||{LF}|#
B|000782|          ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT{LF}|#
B|000783|        );{LF}|#
B|000784|        RAISE;{LF}|#
B|000785|END P_DMF0000_SAVE_EXCHANGE_POPUP_DATA;{LF}|#

B|000793|){LF}|#
B|000794|/*{LF}|#
B|000795| * Program ID   : [DMF0020_EXCEL_UPLOAD]{LF}|#

B|000806| */{LF}|#
B|000807|IS{LF}|#
B|000808|/*{LF}|#

B|000814|0 으로 넣은 경우: Validation 에서 Invalid 처리{LF}|#
B|000815|빈값으로 넣은 경우: SKIP, 기존 Sales Price 데이터를 유지{LF}|#

B|000818|=>2)위 체크조건 통과했을 경우 여러개 Currency 입력방지 위해 중복체크{LF}|#
B|000819|4.ItemID가 입력되지 않은 것들과  ItemID가 입력된 건들을 나누어서 추출 후 병합(row순서는 고려하지 않음){LF}|#
B|000820|=>{LF}|#
B|000821|81{TAB}자동차.마케팅.OTS.OTS2팀{TAB}Corvus Norway{TAB}A22CR1001{TAB}USD{TAB} AMVAGBEVM-A4-D13  300{TAB}300{TAB}300{TAB}300{TAB}300{LF}|#
B|000822|81{TAB}자동차.마케팅.OTS.OTS2팀{TAB}Corvus Norway{TAB}A22CR1001{TAB}USD{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}   100{TAB}100{TAB}100{TAB}100{TAB}100{LF}|#

B|000824|순서로 입력되어 있어도 ItemID가 입력되지 않은 것 먼저 처리함(2024.05.29 박필수 책임 확인사항){LF}|#
B|000825|=>이를 위해 ItemID 없는 건들 먼저 추출(전체 데이터셋){LF}|#

B|000829|*/{LF}|#
B|000830|    V_PROGRAM_ID        VARCHAR2(40 CHAR) := P_PROGRAM_ID; -- 'DMF0020';{LF}|#

B|000946|        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD{LF}|#
B|000947|           SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'{LF}|#
B|000948|             -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-12 빈 값 판정) (START){LF}|#
B|000949|--             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = CASE WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE1, '') = '' THEN 'NULL DIVISION.'{LF}|#
B|000950|--                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE2, '') = '' THEN 'NULL TEAM.'{LF}|#
B|000951|--                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE3, '') = '' THEN 'NULL END CUSTOMER.'{LF}|#
B|000952|--                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE4, '') = '' THEN 'NULL PROJECT CODE.'{LF}|#
B|000953|--                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE5, '') = '' THEN 'NULL CURRENCY.'{LF}|#
B|000954|             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = CASE WHEN ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL THEN 'NULL DIVISION.'{LF}|#
B|000955|                                        WHEN ATTRIBUTE2 IS NULL THEN 'NULL TEAM.'{LF}|#
B|000956|                                        WHEN ATTRIBUTE3 IS NULL THEN 'NULL END CUSTOMER.'{LF}|#
B|000957|                                        WHEN ATTRIBUTE4 IS NULL THEN 'NULL PROJECT CODE.'{LF}|#
B|000958|                                        WHEN ATTRIBUTE5 IS NULL THEN 'NULL CURRENCY.'{LF}|#
B|000959|             -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-12 빈 값 판정) (END){LF}|#
B|000960|                                   END{LF}|#
B|000961|         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID{LF}|#
B|000962|           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME{LF}|#
B|000963|           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000964|           AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|000965|           AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'{LF}|#
B|000966|           AND ({LF}|#
B|000967|                -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-12 빈 값 판정) (START){LF}|#
B|000968|--                NVL(ATTRIBUTE1, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE2, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE3, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE4, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE5, '') = ''{LF}|#
B|000969|                ATTRIBUTE1 IS NULL OR ATTRIBUTE2 IS NULL OR ATTRIBUTE3 IS NULL OR ATTRIBUTE4 IS NULL OR ATTRIBUTE5 IS NULL{LF}|#
B|000970|                -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-12 빈 값 판정) (END){LF}|#
B|000971|               );{LF}|#
B|000972|        N_LOG_COUNT   := N_LOG_COUNT + 1;{LF}|#

B|000978|        /* #################################################### */{LF}|#
B|000979|        /* 동일아이템 중복 체크{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000980|        /* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000981|        /* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000982|        /* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000983|        /* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000984|        /* ATTRIBUTE5 : Currency{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000985|        /* ATTRIBUTE6 : Model{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000986|        /* #################################################### */{LF}|#
B|000987|        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD{LF}|#
B|000988|           SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'{LF}|#
B|000989|             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = 'Duplication Model.'{LF}|#
B|000990|         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID{LF}|#
B|000991|           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME{LF}|#
B|000992|           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|000993|           AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|000994|           AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'{LF}|#
B|000995|           AND EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|000996|                SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|000997|                FROM ({LF}|#
B|000998|                    SELECT X.ATTRIBUTE1 AS COL1/* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|000999|                         , X.ATTRIBUTE2 AS COL2/* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001000|                         , X.ATTRIBUTE3 AS COL3/* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001001|                         , X.ATTRIBUTE4 AS COL4/* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001002|                         , X.ATTRIBUTE6 AS COL6/*ATTRIBUTE6  : Model{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001003|                      FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X{LF}|#
B|001004|                     WHERE X.PROGRAM_ID    = V_PROGRAM_ID{LF}|#
B|001005|                       AND X.SHEET_NAME    = V_SHEET_NAME{LF}|#
B|001006|                       AND X.CREATED_BY    = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|001007|                       AND TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|001008|                       AND X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'{LF}|#
B|001009|                     GROUP BY X.ATTRIBUTE1, X.ATTRIBUTE2, X.ATTRIBUTE3, X.ATTRIBUTE4, X.ATTRIBUTE6{LF}|#
B|001010|                    HAVING COUNT(*) > 1{LF}|#
B|001011|                ) T{LF}|#
B|001012|                WHERE T.COL1 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1{LF}|#
B|001013|                  AND T.COL2 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2{LF}|#
B|001014|                  AND T.COL3 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE3{LF}|#
B|001015|                  AND T.COL4 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE4{LF}|#
B|001016|                  AND T.COL6 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE6{LF}|#
B|001017|               );{LF}|#
B|001018|        ------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001019|        --scm2_mgr.xLOG('ERROR Key Duplication ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 4, TRUE);{LF}|#
B|001020|        V_LOG_TYPE    := '4';{LF}|#
B|001021|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Model Duplication ##########';{LF}|#
B|001022|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#

B|001025|        ------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001026|        /* #################################################### */{LF}|#
B|001027|        /* 위 체크 통과했을 경우 Currency 체크{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001028|        /*두 개 이상의 Currency가 중복으로 입력되면 안됨{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001029|        /* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001030|        /* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001031|        /* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001032|        /* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001033|        /* ATTRIBUTE5 : Currency{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001034|        /* ATTRIBUTE6 : Model{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}*/{LF}|#
B|001035|        /* #################################################### */{LF}|#
B|001036|        DECLARE{LF}|#
B|001037|            V_MODEL_DUP_EXISTS PLS_INTEGER;{LF}|#
B|001038|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|001039|            SELECT 1 INTO V_MODEL_DUP_EXISTS FROM DUAL WHERE EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|001040|                SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|001041|                  FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X{LF}|#
B|001042|                 WHERE X.PROGRAM_ID    = V_PROGRAM_ID{LF}|#
B|001043|                   AND X.SHEET_NAME    = V_SHEET_NAME{LF}|#
B|001044|                   AND X.CREATED_BY    = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|001045|                   AND TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|001046|                   AND X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'{LF}|#
B|001047|                 GROUP BY X.ATTRIBUTE1, X.ATTRIBUTE2, X.ATTRIBUTE3, X.ATTRIBUTE4, X.ATTRIBUTE6{LF}|#
B|001048|                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1{LF}|#
B|001049|            );{LF}|#
B|001050|            -- 모델 중복이 존재하면 Currency 체크 skip{LF}|#
B|001051|        EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|001052|            WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN{LF}|#
B|001053|                -- 모델 중복 없을 때만 Multiple Currency 체크{LF}|#
B|001054|                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD{LF}|#
B|001055|                   SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'{LF}|#
B|001056|                     , MESSAGE_TEXT       = 'Multiple Currency.'{LF}|#
B|001057|                 WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID{LF}|#
B|001058|                   AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME{LF}|#
B|001059|                   AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|001060|                   AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|001061|                   AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'{LF}|#
B|001062|                   AND EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|001063|                        SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|001064|                        FROM ({LF}|#
B|001065|                            SELECT COL1, COL2, COL3, COL4{LF}|#
B|001066|                            FROM ({LF}|#
B|001067|                                SELECT X.ATTRIBUTE1 AS COL1{LF}|#
B|001068|                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE2 AS COL2{LF}|#
B|001069|                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE3 AS COL3{LF}|#
B|001070|                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE4 AS COL4{LF}|#
B|001071|                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE5 AS COL5{LF}|#
B|001072|                                  FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X{LF}|#
B|001073|                                 WHERE X.PROGRAM_ID    = V_PROGRAM_ID{LF}|#
B|001074|                                   AND X.SHEET_NAME    = V_SHEET_NAME{LF}|#
B|001075|                                   AND X.CREATED_BY    = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|001076|                                   AND TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|001077|                                   AND X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'{LF}|#
B|001078|                                 GROUP BY X.ATTRIBUTE1, X.ATTRIBUTE2, X.ATTRIBUTE3, X.ATTRIBUTE4, X.ATTRIBUTE5{LF}|#
B|001079|                            ) A{LF}|#
B|001080|                            GROUP BY COL1, COL2, COL3, COL4{LF}|#
B|001081|                           HAVING COUNT(*) > 1{LF}|#
B|001082|                        ) T{LF}|#
B|001083|                        WHERE T.COL1 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1{LF}|#
B|001084|                          AND T.COL2 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2{LF}|#
B|001085|                          AND T.COL3 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE3{LF}|#
B|001086|                          AND T.COL4 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE4{LF}|#
B|001087|                       );{LF}|#
B|001088|        END;{LF}|#
B|001089|        ------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001090|        --scm2_mgr.xLOG('ERROR Key Duplication ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 4, TRUE);{LF}|#
B|001091|        V_LOG_TYPE    := '4';{LF}|#
B|001092|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Multiple Currency ##########';{LF}|#
B|001093|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|001094|        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|001095|        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#

B|001780|            );{LF}|#
B|001781|            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|001782|                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_WITH_ITEM',{LF}|#
B|001783|                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'{LF}|#
B|001784|            );{LF}|#
B|001785|            ----==============================================================================={LF}|#
B|001786|            --두 데이터셋 합치기{LF}|#
B|001787|            /* ITEM있는 항목(GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_WITH_ITEM)의 값이 '0'이면 원래값 유지*/{LF}|#
B|001788|            MERGE INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP1 A{LF}|#
B|001789|            USING GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_WITH_ITEM B{LF}|#
B|001790|               ON ({LF}|#
B|001791|                    A.DIVISION_ID  = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|001792|                AND A.SCM_SITE_ID  = B.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|001793|                AND A.PROJECT_CODE = B.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|001794|                AND A.ITEM_ID      = B.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|001795|                AND A.CURRENCY     = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|001796|                AND A.INCOTERMS    = B.INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|001797|                AND A.DOM_EXP      = B.DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|001798|                AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|001799|                AND A.FCST_TYPE    = B.FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|001800|                  ){LF}|#
B|001801|            WHEN MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|001802|                UPDATE SET{LF}|#
B|001803|                    A.M0  = CASE WHEN B.M0  = '0' THEN A.M0  ELSE B.M0  END{LF}|#
B|001804|                  , A.M1  = CASE WHEN B.M1  = '0' THEN A.M1  ELSE B.M1  END{LF}|#
B|001805|                  , A.M2  = CASE WHEN B.M2  = '0' THEN A.M2  ELSE B.M2  END{LF}|#
B|001806|                  , A.M3  = CASE WHEN B.M3  = '0' THEN A.M3  ELSE B.M3  END{LF}|#
B|001807|                  , A.M4  = CASE WHEN B.M4  = '0' THEN A.M4  ELSE B.M4  END{LF}|#
B|001808|                  , A.M5  = CASE WHEN B.M5  = '0' THEN A.M5  ELSE B.M5  END{LF}|#
B|001809|                  , A.M6  = CASE WHEN B.M6  = '0' THEN A.M6  ELSE B.M6  END{LF}|#
B|001810|                  , A.M7  = CASE WHEN B.M7  = '0' THEN A.M7  ELSE B.M7  END{LF}|#
B|001811|                  , A.M8  = CASE WHEN B.M8  = '0' THEN A.M8  ELSE B.M8  END{LF}|#
B|001812|                  , A.M9  = CASE WHEN B.M9  = '0' THEN A.M9  ELSE B.M9  END{LF}|#
B|001813|                  , A.M10 = CASE WHEN B.M10 = '0' THEN A.M10 ELSE B.M10 END{LF}|#
B|001814|                  , A.M11 = CASE WHEN B.M11 = '0' THEN A.M11 ELSE B.M11 END{LF}|#
B|001815|                  , A.M12 = CASE WHEN B.M12 = '0' THEN A.M12 ELSE B.M12 END{LF}|#
B|001816|            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|001817|                INSERT (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001818|                        INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001819|                        M0, M1, M2, M3, M4, M5, M6, M7, M8, M9, M10, M11, M12){LF}|#
B|001820|                VALUES (B.DIVISION_ID, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, B.ITEM_ID, B.CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001821|                        B.INCOTERMS, B.DOM_EXP, B.PLANT_SITE_ID, B.FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001822|                        B.M0, B.M1, B.M2, B.M3, B.M4, B.M5, B.M6, B.M7, B.M8, B.M9, B.M10, B.M11, B.M12);{LF}|#
B|001823|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001824|            --------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001825|            -- 5. 추출된 PK 기준으로 LOC Measure의 가격을 Upload Master 기준 가격으로 Calendar Join해서 주차별 반영(Insert / Update, 52주 구간){LF}|#
B|001826|            --------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001827|            --월별로 UNPIVOT하여 실제 월 구하기(GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2){LF}|#
B|001828|            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2{LF}|#
B|001829|            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001830|             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2){LF}|#
B|001831|            SELECT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001832|                   INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM,{LF}|#
B|001833|                   CASE MM{LF}|#
B|001834|                       WHEN 'M0'  THEN TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001835|                       WHEN 'M1'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  1), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001836|                       WHEN 'M2'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  2), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001837|                       WHEN 'M3'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  3), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001838|                       WHEN 'M4'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  4), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001839|                       WHEN 'M5'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  5), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001840|                       WHEN 'M6'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  6), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001841|                       WHEN 'M7'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  7), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001842|                       WHEN 'M8'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  8), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001843|                       WHEN 'M9'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  9), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001844|                       WHEN 'M10' THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 10), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001845|                       WHEN 'M11' THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 11), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001846|                       WHEN 'M12' THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 12), 'YYYYMM'){LF}|#
B|001847|                   END AS MM2{LF}|#
B|001848|              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP1{LF}|#
B|001849|            UNPIVOT ({LF}|#
B|001850|                LOC FOR MM IN ({LF}|#
B|001851|                    M0  AS 'M0',  M1  AS 'M1',  M2  AS 'M2',  M3  AS 'M3',{LF}|#
B|001852|                    M4  AS 'M4',  M5  AS 'M5',  M6  AS 'M6',  M7  AS 'M7',{LF}|#
B|001853|                    M8  AS 'M8',  M9  AS 'M9',  M10 AS 'M10', M11 AS 'M11',{LF}|#
B|001854|                    M12 AS 'M12'{LF}|#
B|001855|                ){LF}|#
B|001856|            );{LF}|#
B|001857|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001858|            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|001859|                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2',{LF}|#
B|001860|                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'{LF}|#
B|001861|            );{LF}|#
B|001862|            --5-1)Upload Master와 CALENDAR 테이블 조인하여 주차별로 ROW 생성{LF}|#
B|001863|            --52주차{LF}|#
B|001864|            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL{LF}|#
B|001865|            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001866|             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE){LF}|#
B|001867|            SELECT DISTINCT{LF}|#
B|001868|                TMP.DIVISION_ID, TMP.SCM_SITE_ID, TMP.PROJECT_CODE, TMP.ITEM_ID, TMP.CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001869|                TMP.INCOTERMS, TMP.DOM_EXP, TMP.PLANT_SITE_ID, TMP.FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001870|                TMP.LOC, TMP.MM, TMP.MM2, CAL.BASIS_START_DATE,{LF}|#
B|001871|                'LOC' --엑셀 입력값은 LOC 메저로 간주{LF}|#
B|001872|              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2 TMP{LF}|#
B|001873|             INNER JOIN ({LF}|#
B|001874|                SELECT DISTINCT{LF}|#
B|001875|                    SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE, 1, 6) AS YYYYMM,{LF}|#
B|001876|                    BASIS_START_DATE{LF}|#
B|001877|                  FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M CAL{LF}|#
B|001878|                 WHERE CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMMDD'){LF}|#
B|001879|                   AND CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <  TO_CHAR(SYSDATE + 52 * 7, 'YYYYMMDD'){LF}|#
B|001880|             ) CAL{LF}|#
B|001881|                ON TMP.MM2 = CAL.YYYYMM{LF}|#
B|001882|             WHERE TO_NUMBER(TMP.LOC) > 0; -- 0 이상만 처리 (빈값 제외){LF}|#
B|001883|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001884|            --5-2)환율데이터 생성{LF}|#
B|001885|            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_EXCH{LF}|#
B|001886|            (DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY, EXCHANGE_PRICE){LF}|#
B|001887|            SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, A.UNIT_CURRENCY, A.EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|001888|              FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE A{LF}|#
B|001889|              JOIN GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2 B{LF}|#
B|001890|                ON A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|001891|               AND A.YYYYMMDD    = B.MM2 || '01'{LF}|#
B|001892|               AND A.CURRENCY    = 'KRW';{LF}|#
B|001893|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001894|            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|001895|                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL',{LF}|#
B|001896|                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'{LF}|#
B|001897|            );{LF}|#
B|001898|            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|001899|                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_EXCH',{LF}|#
B|001900|                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'{LF}|#
B|001901|            );{LF}|#
B|001902|            --5-3) CURRENCY가 'KRW', 'USD'일 경우 해당 CURRENCY로 메저생성{LF}|#
B|001903|            --환율 계산할 필요 없으므로 LOC값 그대로 저장{LF}|#
B|001904|            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL{LF}|#
B|001905|            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001906|             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE){LF}|#
B|001907|            SELECT{LF}|#
B|001908|                A.DIVISION_ID, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.ITEM_ID, A.CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001909|                A.INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001910|                A.LOC, A.MM, A.MM2, A.BASIS_START_DATE,{LF}|#
B|001911|                A.CURRENCY AS MEASURE{LF}|#
B|001912|              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL A{LF}|#
B|001913|             WHERE A.MEASURE  = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|001914|               AND A.CURRENCY IN ('KRW', 'USD');{LF}|#
B|001915|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001916|            --5-4) KRW 메저 생성(기준값)  : 환율금액을 곱하여 KRW 생성{LF}|#
B|001917|            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL{LF}|#
B|001918|            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001919|             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE){LF}|#
B|001920|            SELECT{LF}|#
B|001921|                A.DIVISION_ID, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.ITEM_ID, A.CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001922|                A.INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001923|                --[E20260930-DMF0020FXZERO] 원문: TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(A.LOC) * B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 3)),{LF}|#
B|001924|                TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(A.LOC) * NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 0), 3)),   -- 환율 없으면 KRW 0{LF}|#
B|001925|                A.MM, A.MM2, A.BASIS_START_DATE,{LF}|#
B|001926|                'KRW'{LF}|#
B|001927|              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL A{LF}|#
B|001928|              --[E20260930-DMF0020FXZERO] 원문: JOIN GT_F020_EXCH B{LF}|#
B|001929|              LEFT JOIN GT_F020_EXCH B   -- 환율 없는 달도 KRW 행 생성{LF}|#
B|001930|                ON B.UNIT_CURRENCY = A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|001931|               AND B.YYYYMMDD      = A.MM2 || '01'{LF}|#
B|001932|             WHERE A.MEASURE   = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|001933|               AND A.CURRENCY <> 'KRW';{LF}|#
B|001934|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001935|            --5-5) USD 메저 생성 : KRW 기준으로 계산{LF}|#
B|001936|            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL{LF}|#
B|001937|            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001938|             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE){LF}|#
B|001939|            SELECT{LF}|#
B|001940|                A.DIVISION_ID, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.ITEM_ID, A.CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|001941|                A.INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001942|                --[E20260930-DMF0020FXZERO] 원문: TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(A.LOC) / B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 3)),{LF}|#
B|001943|                TO_CHAR(CASE WHEN NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 0) = 0 THEN 0 ELSE ROUND(TO_NUMBER(A.LOC) / B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 3) END),   -- USD 환율 없으면 0{LF}|#
B|001944|                A.MM, A.MM2, A.BASIS_START_DATE,{LF}|#
B|001945|                'USD'{LF}|#
B|001946|              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL A{LF}|#
B|001947|              --[E20260930-DMF0020FXZERO] 원문: JOIN GT_F020_EXCH B{LF}|#
B|001948|              LEFT JOIN GT_F020_EXCH B   -- 환율 없는 달도 USD 행 생성{LF}|#
B|001949|                ON B.UNIT_CURRENCY = 'USD'{LF}|#
B|001950|               AND B.YYYYMMDD      = A.MM2 || '01'{LF}|#
B|001951|             WHERE A.MEASURE   = 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|001952|               AND A.CURRENCY <> 'USD';{LF}|#
B|001953|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|001954|            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|001955|                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL',{LF}|#
B|001956|                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'{LF}|#
B|001957|            );{LF}|#
B|001958|            ----------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001959|            --6) SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 저장{LF}|#
B|001960|            ----------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001961|            MERGE INTO SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE A{LF}|#
B|001962|            USING GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL B{LF}|#
B|001963|               ON ({LF}|#
B|001964|                    A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|001965|                AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = B.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|001966|                AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = B.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|001967|                AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|001968|                AND A.ITEM_ID       = B.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|001969|                AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.BASIS_START_DATE{LF}|#
B|001970|                AND A.CURRENCY      = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|001971|                AND A.MEASURE       = B.MEASURE{LF}|#
B|001972|                AND A.DOM_EXP       = B.DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|001973|                AND A.FCST_TYPE     = B.FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|001974|                AND A.INCOTERMS     = B.INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|001975|                  ){LF}|#
B|001976|            WHEN MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|001977|                UPDATE SET{LF}|#
B|001978|                    A.PRICE            = TO_NUMBER(B.LOC){LF}|#
B|001979|                  , A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|001980|                  , A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = SYSDATE{LF}|#
B|001981|                  , A.ATTRIBUTE8       = 'EXCEL UPLOAD' --엑셀업로드 여부 표시{LF}|#
B|001982|            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|001983|                INSERT ({LF}|#
B|001984|                    DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID,{LF}|#
B|001985|                    ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|001986|                    INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE,{LF}|#
B|001987|                    LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE, ATTRIBUTE8{LF}|#
B|001988|                ){LF}|#
B|001989|                VALUES ({LF}|#
B|001990|                    NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.DIVISION_ID),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.PROJECT_CODE),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.SCM_SITE_ID),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.PLANT_SITE_ID),''), '/'),{LF}|#
B|001991|                    NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.ITEM_ID),''), '/'), NVL(B.BASIS_START_DATE, '-'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.CURRENCY),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.MEASURE),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.DOM_EXP),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.FCST_TYPE),''), '/'),{LF}|#
B|001992|                    NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.INCOTERMS),''), '/'), TO_NUMBER(B.LOC), P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|001993|                    P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE, 'EXCEL UPLOAD' --엑셀업로드 여부 표시{LF}|#
B|001994|                );{LF}|#
B|001995|            N_LOG_COUNT := SQL%ROWCOUNT;{LF}|#
B|001996|            ----------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001997|            --7) SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H 저장{LF}|#
B|001998|            ----------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|001999|            INSERT INTO SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H{LF}|#
B|002000|            ({LF}|#
B|002001|                DIVISION_ID, HISTORY_SEQ, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID,{LF}|#
B|002002|                ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|002003|                INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE,{LF}|#
B|002004|                LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE, ATTRIBUTE8{LF}|#
B|002005|            ){LF}|#
B|002006|            SELECT{LF}|#
B|002007|                DIVISION_ID,{LF}|#
B|002008|                SEQ1_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H.NEXTVAL,{LF}|#
B|002009|                PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID,{LF}|#
B|002010|                ITEM_ID, BASIS_START_DATE, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE,{LF}|#
B|002011|                INCOTERMS, TO_NUMBER(LOC), P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|002012|                P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|002013|                'EXCEL UPLOAD' --엑셀업로드 여부 표시{LF}|#
B|002014|              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL;{LF}|#
B|002015|            COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|002016|            EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION DISABLE PARALLEL DML';{LF}|#
B|002017|            ----------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|002018|            --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);{LF}|#
B|002019|            V_LOG_TYPE    := '21';{LF}|#
B|002020|            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT DMF0020_EXCEL_UPLOAD END ##########';{LF}|#
B|002021|            V_DESCRIPTION := TO_CHAR(P_MODE);{LF}|#
B|002022|            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002023|            BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|002024|            ----------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|002025|        EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|002026|            WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|002027|                ROLLBACK;{LF}|#
B|002028|                EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION DISABLE PARALLEL DML';{LF}|#
B|002029|                V_SQL_CODE   := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);{LF}|#
B|002030|                V_SQL_ERRMSG := SQLERRM;{LF}|#
B|002031|                V_BACKTRACE  := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;{LF}|#
B|002032|                V_PROC_INPUT := JSON_OBJECT({LF}|#
B|002033|                    'P_MODE'       VALUE P_MODE,{LF}|#
B|002034|                    'P_CREATED_BY' VALUE P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|002035|                );{LF}|#
B|002036|                BEGIN{LF}|#
B|002037|                    SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH({LF}|#
B|002038|                        $$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ERROR', N_LOG_COUNT, 'EXCEPTION',{LF}|#
B|002039|                        '', SYSDATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|002040|                        V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|002041|                    );{LF}|#
B|002042|                END;{LF}|#
B|002043|                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,{LF}|#
B|002044|                    '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||{LF}|#
B|002045|                    ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||{LF}|#
B|002046|                    ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||{LF}|#
B|002047|                    ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT{LF}|#
B|002048|                );{LF}|#
B|002049|                RAISE;{LF}|#
B|002050|        END;{LF}|#
B|002051|    END IF; -- END OF p_mode > 0{LF}|#
B|002052|    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|002053|    --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);{LF}|#
B|002054|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|002055|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT DATA END ##########';{LF}|#
B|002056|    V_DESCRIPTION := TO_CHAR(P_MODE);{LF}|#
B|002057|    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002058|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|002059|    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|002060|    -- 결과 반환 분기{LF}|#
B|002061|    IF NVL(P_MODE, 0) = 0 THEN{LF}|#
B|002062|        -- 조회(Validation) 성공: EVENT는 NULL (Case 3 조회 성공 패턴){LF}|#
B|002063|        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|002064|        SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|002065|    ELSE{LF}|#
B|002066|        -- DML 성공: EVENT에 처리 건수 반환{LF}|#
B|002067|        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|002068|        SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, TO_CHAR(N_LOG_COUNT) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|002069|    END IF;{LF}|#
B|002070|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|002071|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|002072|        ROLLBACK;{LF}|#
B|002073|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|002074|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#
B|002075|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|002076|        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002077|        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);{LF}|#
B|002078|        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;{LF}|#
B|002079|        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT({LF}|#
B|002080|            'P_MODE'       VALUE P_MODE,{LF}|#
B|002081|            'P_CREATED_BY' VALUE P_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|002082|        );{LF}|#
B|002083|        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;{LF}|#
B|002084|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|002085|            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH({LF}|#
B|002086|                $$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE,{LF}|#
B|002087|                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|002088|                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|002089|            );{LF}|#
B|002090|        END;{LF}|#
B|002091|        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,{LF}|#
B|002092|            '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||{LF}|#
B|002093|            ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||{LF}|#
B|002094|            ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||{LF}|#
B|002095|            ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT{LF}|#
B|002096|        );{LF}|#
B|002097|        RAISE;{LF}|#
B|002098|END P_DMF0020_EXCEL_UPLOAD;{LF}|#
B|002099|PROCEDURE        P_DMF0020_GET_SPREAD_TITLE{LF}|#
B|002100|({LF}|#
B|002101|    P_START_DT     IN VARCHAR2,{LF}|#
B|002102|    P_END_DT       IN VARCHAR2,{LF}|#
B|002103|    P_CREATED_BY   IN VARCHAR2,{LF}|#
B|002104|    P_DIVISION_ID  IN VARCHAR2,{LF}|#
B|002105|    O_RC1          OUT SYS_REFCURSOR,{LF}|#
B|002106|    O_RESULT_CURSOR OUT SYS_REFCURSOR{LF}|#
B|002107|){LF}|#
B|002108|AS{LF}|#
B|002109|/****************************************************************************************************/{LF}|#
B|002110|/* Program ID  : DMF0020_GET_SPREAD_TITLE                                                             */{LF}|#
B|002111|/* Description : Procedure 작성 가이드                                                              */{LF}|#
B|002112|/* Usage       : Procedure 작성 시 참조하세요!                                                      */{LF}|#
B|002113|/* Parameter   :                                                                                    */{LF}|#
B|002114|/*                                                                                                  */{LF}|#
B|002115|/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/{LF}|#
B|002116|/* Date        In Charge    Description                                                             */{LF}|#

B|002421|            FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M{LF}|#
B|002422|            WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= P_START_DT{LF}|#
B|002423|                AND BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_DT{LF}|#
B|002424|                AND CALENDAR_DAY = '01'{LF}|#
B|002425|            GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, WEEK_NAME, SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE, 5, 4), MONTH_NAME{LF}|#
B|002426|        ) MA{LF}|#
B|002427|        ORDER BY SORT_ORDER_NO, COLUMN_LABEL_NAME, COLUMN_NAME;{LF}|#
B|002428|    END IF;{LF}|#
B|002429|    /*-- 날짜 부분 -------------------------------{LF}|#
B|002430|    SELECT{LF}|#
B|002431|        'W_' || MIN(BASIS_START_DATE) AS BASIS_START_DATE2,{LF}|#
B|002432|        MIN(BASIS_START_DATE) AS BASIS_START_DATE, BASIS_YYYYMM,{LF}|#
B|002433|        WEEK_NAME, SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE,5,4) AS MMDD{LF}|#
B|002434|        , CALENDAR_YYYY AS YER, CALENDAR_MONTH AS MON{LF}|#
B|002435|    FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M{LF}|#
B|002436|    WHERE{LF}|#
B|002437|        BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= P_START_DT AND BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_DT{LF}|#
B|002438|    GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, WEEK_NAME, SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE,5,4), CALENDAR_YYYY, CALENDAR_MONTH{LF}|#
B|002439|    UNION ALL{LF}|#
B|002440|    SELECT  'M_' || MIN(BASIS_START_DATE) AS BASIS_START_DATE2,{LF}|#
B|002441|        MIN(BASIS_START_DATE) AS BASIS_START_DATE{LF}|#
B|002442|        , BASIS_YYYYMM,{LF}|#
B|002443|        WEEK_NAME, SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE,5,4) AS MMDD{LF}|#
B|002444|        , CALENDAR_YYYY AS YER, CALENDAR_MONTH AS MON{LF}|#
B|002445|    FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M{LF}|#
B|002446|    WHERE{LF}|#
B|002447|        BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= P_START_DT AND BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_END_DT{LF}|#
B|002448|        AND CALENDAR_DAY = '01'{LF}|#
B|002449|    GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, WEEK_NAME, SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE,5,4), CALENDAR_YYYY, CALENDAR_MONTH{LF}|#
B|002450|    ORDER BY YER, MMDD, BASIS_START_DATE2 --DESC{LF}|#
B|002451|    -- 날짜 부분 -------------------------------{LF}|#
B|002452|    */{LF}|#
B|002453|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002454|    -- 조회 성공 O_RESULT_CURSOR (Case 3 표준 패턴){LF}|#
B|002455|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002456|    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|002457|    SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|002458|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002459|    -- END 로그{LF}|#
B|002460|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002461|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|002462|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#

B|002466|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|002467|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#

B|002469|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|002470|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#
B|002471|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#

B|002494|        );{LF}|#

B|002497|PROCEDURE P_DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE{LF}|#

B|002518|/*{LF}|#
