B|002523|PRINT RC2{LF}|#
B|002524|*/{LF}|#
B|002525|    -- EXCEPTION 블록 필수 변수{LF}|#
B|002526|    V_PROC_INPUT    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002527|    V_BACKTRACE     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002528|    V_SQL_CODE      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002529|    V_SQL_ERRMSG    VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002530|    V_LOG_TYPE      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002531|    V_LOG_MESSAGE   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002532|    V_DESCRIPTION   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|002533|    N_LOG_COUNT     PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#
B|002534|    D_EVENT_DATE    DATE := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002535|BEGIN{LF}|#
B|002536|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002537|    -- START 로그{LF}|#
B|002538|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002539|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'START';{LF}|#
B|002540|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## START of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|002541|    V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|002542|    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002543|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|002544|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002545|    -- 메인 조회{LF}|#
B|002546|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002547|    OPEN O_RC1 FOR{LF}|#
B|002548|    WITH TEMP_VERSION_NO AS ({LF}|#
B|002549|        --DIV별 최신 버전(2024.10.04)---------------------{LF}|#
B|002550|        SELECT /*+ MATERIALIZE */{LF}|#
B|002551|               A.DIVISION_ID, A.VERSION_NO, A.YYYYMMDD, B.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM{LF}|#
B|002552|        FROM{LF}|#
B|002553|        ({LF}|#
B|002554|            SELECT DIVISION_ID, MAX(VERSION_NO) AS VERSION_NO, MAX(YYYYMMDD) AS YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|002555|            FROM DM_MST_VERSION{LF}|#
B|002556|            WHERE DIVISION_ID = P_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002557|            AND VERSION_TYPE = 'SP_W'{LF}|#
B|002558|            --AND NVL(ATTRIBUTE7, 'N') = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002559|            GROUP BY DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002560|        ) A{LF}|#
B|002561|        INNER JOIN SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B{LF}|#
B|002562|                ON B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = A.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|002563|    ){LF}|#
B|002564|    SELECT DISTINCT{LF}|#
B|002565|        A.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002566|        , A.DEPT_NAME_KO AS DEPT_NAME{LF}|#
B|002567|        , A.END_CUSTOMER{LF}|#
B|002568|        , A.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002569|        , MAX(A.CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY -- Model별로 하나의 CURRENCY만 있어야 하므로 MAX값 취함{LF}|#
B|002570|        , A.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|002571|        , '' AS M0{LF}|#
B|002572|        , '' AS M1{LF}|#
B|002573|        , '' AS M2{LF}|#
B|002574|        , '' AS M3{LF}|#
B|002575|        , '' AS M4{LF}|#
B|002576|        , '' AS M5{LF}|#
B|002577|        , '' AS M6{LF}|#
B|002578|        , '' AS M7{LF}|#
B|002579|        , '' AS M8{LF}|#
B|002580|        , '' AS M9{LF}|#
B|002581|        , '' AS M10{LF}|#
B|002582|        , '' AS M11{LF}|#
B|002583|        , '' AS M12{LF}|#
B|002584|        , EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002585|    FROM{LF}|#
B|002586|    ({LF}|#
B|002587|        SELECT{LF}|#
B|002588|        SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002589|        , TEAM.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002590|        , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO{LF}|#
B|002591|        , MS.END_CUSTOMER{LF}|#
B|002592|        , SP.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002593|        , MP.PROJECT_NAME{LF}|#
B|002594|        , SP.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|002595|        , SP.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002596|        -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-추가 통화 대체 판정) (START){LF}|#
B|002597|--        , CASE WHEN NVL(SP.CURRENCY, '') = '' THEN SI.CURRENCY ELSE SP.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY--CURRENCY가 없을 경우 SSPM의 값을 가져옴{LF}|#
B|002598|        , CASE WHEN SP.CURRENCY IS NULL THEN SI.CURRENCY ELSE SP.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY--CURRENCY가 없을 경우 SSPM의 값을 가져옴{LF}|#
B|002599|        -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-추가 통화 대체 판정) (END){LF}|#
B|002600|        , NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002601|        FROM DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_C SP{LF}|#
B|002602|            INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002603|                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V{LF}|#
B|002604|                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID{LF}|#
B|002605|                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|002606|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#
B|002607|            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID{LF}|#
B|002608|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE MS{LF}|#
B|002609|            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002610|            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002611|            AND MS.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002612|        -- 변경된 DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002613|        -- [hook#150-MAX 2026-09-07] AS-IS: INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM IM{LF}|#
B|002614|            -- [hook#150-MAX 2026-09-07] AS-IS: ON MS.DIVISION_ID = IM.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002615|            -- [hook#150-MAX 2026-09-07] AS-IS: AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = IM.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002616|        -- 2026-09-07 hook#150: site 대표부서 = (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID) 기준 MAX(DEPT_ID) 1건 (룰 §3-1, 사용자 지시).{LF}|#
B|002617|        --   AS-IS 는 IM 을 DIV+SITE 단독 INNER JOIN 하여 SITE_ITEM 행수만큼 증식한다(실측: 키당 최대 3,506행).{LF}|#
B|002618|        --   DI 는 유효부서 필터 전용(출력 DEPT_ID 는 PK grain 의 TEAM 에서 나온다)이므로{LF}|#
B|002619|        --   MAX 를 유효부서 집합 '안에서' 취해 통과 키 집합을 보존한다(룰 §3-4 / G150-7).{LF}|#
B|002620|        INNER JOIN (SELECT SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(SA.DEPT_ID) AS DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002621|                      FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SA{LF}|#
B|002622|                     INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO SD{LF}|#
B|002623|                        ON SD.DEPT_ID  = SA.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002624|                       AND SD.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002625|                     WHERE SA.DEPT_ID IS NOT NULL{LF}|#
B|002626|                     GROUP BY SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID) IM{LF}|#
B|002627|            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = IM.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002628|            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = IM.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002629|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO DI{LF}|#
B|002630|            -- ON DI.DEPT_ID = MS.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002631|            ON DI.DEPT_ID = IM.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002632|            AND DI.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002633|        /* DEPT_ID 테이블 변경{LF}|#
B|002634|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO DI{LF}|#
B|002635|            ON DI.DEPT_ID = MS.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002636|            AND DI.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002637|        */{LF}|#
B|002638|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_PROJECT MP{LF}|#
B|002639|            ON MP.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002640|            AND MP.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE --2026-07-03 perf: AS-IS 원문 활성 조인 복원 (주석 시 사업부 단위 준-카티전 → 86s/60행){LF}|#
B|002641|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SI{LF}|#
B|002642|            ON SI.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002643|            AND SI.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002644|            AND SI.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002645|            AND SI.ITEM_ID = SP.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|002646|            AND SI.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002647|        INNER JOIN TEMP_VERSION_NO T_VER--DIV별 최신버전(2024.10.04){LF}|#
B|002648|            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T_VER.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002649|            AND SP.VERSION_NO = T_VER.VERSION_NO{LF}|#
B|002650|        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: LEFT JOIN ({LF}|#
B|002651|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: SELECT B.USER_ID, B.USER_NAME, B.USER_NAME_EN, B.DEPT_ID, C.DEPT_NAME_KO, C.UPPER_DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002652|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: FROM SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO B{LF}|#
B|002653|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO C{LF}|#
B|002654|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON B.DEPT_ID = C.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002655|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ) TEAM{LF}|#
B|002656|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON{LF}|#
B|002657|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: TEAM.USER_ID = TRIM({LF}|#
B|002658|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: CASE{LF}|#
B|002659|                    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: WHEN INSTR(SI.OWNER, ',') > 0{LF}|#
B|002660|                    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: THEN SUBSTR(SI.OWNER, 1, INSTR(SI.OWNER, ',') - 1){LF}|#
B|002661|                    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ELSE SI.OWNER{LF}|#
B|002662|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: END{LF}|#
B|002663|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ){LF}|#
B|002664|        -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS: DEPT_ID 원천 = SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.DEPT_ID (SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 삭제 예정 / OWNER 경유 폐기){LF}|#
B|002665|        LEFT JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM{LF}|#
B|002666|            ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = SI.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002667|        WHERE SP.DIVISION_ID = P_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002668|        AND SP.QTY <> 0{LF}|#
B|002669|        AND SP.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP'{LF}|#
B|002670|        --버전의 날짜로 처리(2024.10.04){LF}|#
B|002671|        AND SP.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(T_VER.YYYYMMDD, 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD'){LF}|#
B|002672|        AND SP.YYYYMMDD <= TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(T_VER.YYYYMMDD, 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'YYYYMMDD') + (52 * 7) - 1, 'YYYYMMDD'){LF}|#
B|002673|        UNION ALL{LF}|#
B|002674|        SELECT{LF}|#
B|002675|        SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002676|        , TEAM.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002677|        , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO{LF}|#
B|002678|        , MS.END_CUSTOMER{LF}|#
B|002679|        , SP.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002680|        , MP.PROJECT_NAME{LF}|#
B|002681|        , SP.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|002682|        , SP.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002683|        -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-추가 통화 대체 판정) (START){LF}|#
B|002684|--        , CASE WHEN NVL(SP.CURRENCY, '') = '' THEN SI.CURRENCY ELSE SP.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY--CURRENCY가 없을 경우 SSPM의 값을 가져옴{LF}|#
B|002685|        , CASE WHEN SP.CURRENCY IS NULL THEN SI.CURRENCY ELSE SP.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY--CURRENCY가 없을 경우 SSPM의 값을 가져옴{LF}|#
B|002686|        -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-추가 통화 대체 판정) (END){LF}|#
B|002687|        , NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002688|        FROM DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT_C SP{LF}|#
B|002689|        INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002690|                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V{LF}|#
B|002691|                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID{LF}|#
B|002692|                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|002693|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#
B|002694|            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID{LF}|#
B|002695|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE MS{LF}|#
B|002696|            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002697|            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002698|            AND MS.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002699|        -- [hook#150-MAX 2026-09-07] AS-IS: INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM IM{LF}|#
B|002700|            -- [hook#150-MAX 2026-09-07] AS-IS: ON MS.DIVISION_ID = IM.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002701|            -- [hook#150-MAX 2026-09-07] AS-IS: AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = IM.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002702|        -- 2026-09-07 hook#150: site 대표부서 = (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID) 기준 MAX(DEPT_ID) 1건 (룰 §3-1, 사용자 지시).{LF}|#
B|002703|        --   AS-IS 는 IM 을 DIV+SITE 단독 INNER JOIN 하여 SITE_ITEM 행수만큼 증식한다(실측: 키당 최대 3,506행).{LF}|#
B|002704|        --   DI 는 유효부서 필터 전용(출력 DEPT_ID 는 PK grain 의 TEAM 에서 나온다)이므로{LF}|#
B|002705|        --   MAX 를 유효부서 집합 '안에서' 취해 통과 키 집합을 보존한다(룰 §3-4 / G150-7).{LF}|#
B|002706|        INNER JOIN (SELECT SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(SA.DEPT_ID) AS DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002707|                      FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SA{LF}|#
B|002708|                     INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO SD{LF}|#
B|002709|                        ON SD.DEPT_ID  = SA.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002710|                       AND SD.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002711|                     WHERE SA.DEPT_ID IS NOT NULL{LF}|#
B|002712|                     GROUP BY SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID) IM{LF}|#
B|002713|            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = IM.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002714|            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = IM.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002715|        -- 변경된 DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002716|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO DI{LF}|#
B|002717|            -- ON DI.DEPT_ID = MS.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002718|            ON DI.DEPT_ID = IM.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002719|            AND DI.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002720|        /* DEPT_ID 테이블 변경{LF}|#
B|002721|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO DI{LF}|#
B|002722|            ON DI.DEPT_ID = MS.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002723|            AND DI.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002724|        */{LF}|#
B|002725|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_PROJECT MP{LF}|#
B|002726|            ON MP.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002727|            AND MP.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002728|        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SI{LF}|#
B|002729|            ON SI.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002730|            AND SI.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002731|            AND SI.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002732|            AND SI.ITEM_ID = SP.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|002733|            AND SI.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002734|        INNER JOIN TEMP_VERSION_NO T_VER--DIV별 최신버전(2024.10.04){LF}|#
B|002735|            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T_VER.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002736|            AND SP.VERSION_NO = T_VER.VERSION_NO{LF}|#
B|002737|        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: LEFT JOIN ({LF}|#
B|002738|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: SELECT B.USER_ID, B.USER_NAME, B.USER_NAME_EN, B.DEPT_ID, C.DEPT_NAME_KO, C.UPPER_DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002739|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: FROM SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO B{LF}|#
B|002740|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO C{LF}|#
B|002741|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON B.DEPT_ID = C.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002742|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ) TEAM{LF}|#
B|002743|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON{LF}|#
B|002744|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: TEAM.USER_ID = TRIM({LF}|#
B|002745|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: CASE{LF}|#
B|002746|                    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: WHEN INSTR(SI.OWNER, ',') > 0{LF}|#
B|002747|                    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: THEN SUBSTR(SI.OWNER, 1, INSTR(SI.OWNER, ',') - 1){LF}|#
B|002748|                    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ELSE SI.OWNER{LF}|#
B|002749|                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: END{LF}|#
B|002750|            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ){LF}|#
B|002751|        -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS: DEPT_ID 원천 = SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.DEPT_ID (SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 삭제 예정 / OWNER 경유 폐기){LF}|#
B|002752|        LEFT JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM{LF}|#
B|002753|            ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = SI.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002754|        WHERE SP.DIVISION_ID = P_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002755|        AND SP.QTY <> 0{LF}|#
B|002756|        AND SP.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP'{LF}|#
B|002757|        AND SP.YYYYMMDD >= T_VER.YYYYMM || '01'{LF}|#
B|002758|        AND SP.YYYYMMDD < TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(T_VER.YYYYMMDD, 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD'){LF}|#
B|002759|    ) A{LF}|#
B|002760|    GROUP BY{LF}|#
B|002761|    A.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002762|    , A.DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|002763|    , A.DEPT_NAME_KO{LF}|#
B|002764|    , A.END_CUSTOMER{LF}|#
B|002765|    , A.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|002766|    , A.PROJECT_NAME{LF}|#
B|002767|    , A.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|002768|    , A.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|002769|    , A.EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002770|    ;{LF}|#
B|002771|    --, A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|002772|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002773|    -- 조회 성공 O_RESULT_CURSOR (Case 3 표준 패턴){LF}|#
B|002774|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002775|    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|002776|    SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|002777|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002778|    -- END 로그{LF}|#
B|002779|    -- ================================================================{LF}|#
B|002780|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|002781|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|002782|    V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|002783|    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002784|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;{LF}|#
B|002785|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|002786|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|002787|        ROLLBACK;{LF}|#
B|002788|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|002789|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#
B|002790|        V_DESCRIPTION := '';{LF}|#
B|002791|        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002792|        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);{LF}|#
B|002793|        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;{LF}|#
B|002794|        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT({LF}|#
B|002795|            'P_DIVISION_ID' VALUE P_DIVISION_ID,{LF}|#
B|002796|            'P_USER_ID'   VALUE P_USER_ID,{LF}|#
B|002797|            'P_MNU_ID'   VALUE P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|002798|        );{LF}|#
B|002799|        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;{LF}|#
B|002800|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|002801|            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH({LF}|#
B|002802|                $$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE,{LF}|#
B|002803|                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|002804|                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|002805|            );{LF}|#
B|002806|        END;{LF}|#
B|002807|        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,{LF}|#
B|002808|          '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||{LF}|#
B|002809|          ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||{LF}|#
B|002810|          ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||{LF}|#
B|002811|          ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT{LF}|#
B|002812|        );{LF}|#
B|002813|        RAISE;{LF}|#
B|002814|END P_DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE;{LF}|#

B|002856|         , CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|002857|         , UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#

B|002859|         , NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN{LF}|#

B|002861|        INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN{LF}|#
B|002862|                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V{LF}|#
B|002863|                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID{LF}|#
B|002864|                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|002865|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#

B|002868|    AND    YYYYMMDD <= P_TO_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#

B|002875|               AND    VALID_YN    = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|002876|           ){LF}|#

B|002881|         , CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|002882|         , UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|002883|         , CURRENCY || '/' || UNIT_CURRENCY AS CURRENCY_UNITCURRENCY{LF}|#
B|002884|         , NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN{LF}|#

B|002887|                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V{LF}|#

B|002890|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#

B|002893|    AND    YYYYMMDD <= P_TO_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#

B|002904|               WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DMF0020_CURRENCY'{LF}|#
B|002905|               AND    VALID_YN    = 'Y'{LF}|#

B|002915|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|002916|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|002917|        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|002918|        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#

B|002982|    D_EVENT_DATE     DATE        := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|002983|BEGIN{LF}|#
B|002984|    -- CASE WHEN 'ALL' 패턴 → UNION ALL 분기 (Case 17){LF}|#

B|002986|    SELECT DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|002987|         , CURRENCY{LF}|#

B|002995|                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID{LF}|#
B|002996|                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID{LF}|#
B|002997|                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2{LF}|#
B|002998|        ON T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID{LF}|#
B|002999|    WHERE  YYYYMMDD >= P_FROM_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003000|    AND    YYYYMMDD <= P_TO_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003001|    AND    P_DIVISION_ID = 'ALL'{LF}|#

B|003003|    AND    UNIT_CURRENCY IN ({LF}|#
B|003004|               SELECT SEGMENT1{LF}|#
B|003005|               FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A{LF}|#
B|003006|               WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DMF0020_CURRENCY'{LF}|#

B|003025|    AND    YYYYMMDD <= P_TO_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003026|    AND    P_DIVISION_ID <> 'ALL'{LF}|#

B|003030|               CONNECT BY REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_DIVISION_ID, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) IS NOT NULL{LF}|#

B|003043|    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#

B|003064|                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|003065|                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|003066|            );{LF}|#
B|003067|        END;{LF}|#

B|003560|    /*{LF}|#

B|003563|    --GET_ADMIN_GROUP{LF}|#
B|003564|    --DM_OWNER_DEPT{LF}|#
B|003565|    */{LF}|#
B|003566|    --쿼리 전체 수정{LF}|#
B|003567|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|003568|    --            20200311 KHSONG C20200103-000586 권한그룹을 LOOKUP관리 (BEGIN){LF}|#
B|003569|    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------{LF}|#
B|003570|    v_ADMIN_GROUP    T_STR_TAB := T_STR_TAB();{LF}|#
B|003571|    v_group_id       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) := '';{LF}|#
B|003572|    T_LEVEL2_DEPT_ID T_STR_TAB := T_STR_TAB();  -- 전지본부의 LEVEL2 아이디가 들어간다.{LF}|#
B|003573|    T_LEVEL1_DEPT_ID T_STR_TAB := T_STR_TAB();  -- 기타 추가하고자 하는 부서의 아이디가 들어간다.{LF}|#
B|003574|    V_TMPDEPT        T_STR_TAB := T_STR_TAB();{LF}|#
B|003575|    V_PROC_INPUT     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003576|    V_BACKTRACE      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003577|    V_SQL_CODE       VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003578|    V_SQL_ERRMSG     VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003579|    V_LOG_TYPE       VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003580|    V_LOG_MESSAGE    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003581|    V_DESCRIPTION    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|003582|    N_LOG_COUNT      PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#
B|003583|    D_EVENT_DATE     DATE        := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|003584|BEGIN{LF}|#
B|003585|    SELECT SEGMENT1{LF}|#
B|003586|    BULK COLLECT INTO v_ADMIN_GROUP{LF}|#
B|003587|    FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL{LF}|#
B|003588|    WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP'{LF}|#
B|003589|    AND    VALID_YN    = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|003590|    AND    SEGMENT2    = 'ALL'{LF}|#
B|003591|    AND    SEGMENT1    = 'ADMIN'; --조건 빠지면 안됨{LF}|#
B|003592|    SELECT SEGMENT1{LF}|#
B|003593|    BULK COLLECT INTO T_LEVEL2_DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|003594|    FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL{LF}|#
B|003595|    WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_OWNER_DEPT'{LF}|#
B|003596|    AND    VALID_YN    = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|003597|    AND    SEGMENT2    = 'LEVEL_2';{LF}|#
B|003598|    SELECT SEGMENT1{LF}|#
B|003599|    BULK COLLECT INTO T_LEVEL1_DEPT_ID{LF}|#
B|003600|    FROM   SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL{LF}|#
B|003601|    WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_OWNER_DEPT'{LF}|#
B|003602|    AND    VALID_YN    = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|003603|    AND    SEGMENT2    = 'LEVEL_1';{LF}|#
B|003604|    -- GroupID JOIN 조건에 사용{LF}|#
B|003605|    v_ADMIN_GROUP.EXTEND(2);{LF}|#
B|003606|    v_ADMIN_GROUP(v_ADMIN_GROUP.COUNT - 1) := 'DM Planner';{LF}|#
B|003607|    v_ADMIN_GROUP(v_ADMIN_GROUP.COUNT)     := 'DM Planner (W/S)'; -- [C20220228-000147]{LF}|#

B|003622|    END IF;{LF}|#

B|003649|                   ){LF}|#

B|003901|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_DIVISION_ID,     '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS DIVISION_ID   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003902|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_YYYYMMDD,        '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS YYYYMMDD   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003903|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_CURRENCY,        '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS CURRENCY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003904|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_UNIT_CURRENCY,   '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS UNIT_CURRENCY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003905|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_EXCHANGE_PRICE,  '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS EXCHANGE_PRICE   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003906|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_CREATED_BY,      '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS CREATED_BY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003907|         -- , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_LAST_UPDATED_BY, '[^\^]+', 1, LEVEL)) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 원본{LF}|#
B|003908|    SELECT TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_ACTIVE_FLAG, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS ACTIVE_FLAG   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003909|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_DIVISION_ID, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS DIVISION_ID   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003910|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_YYYYMMDD, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS YYYYMMDD   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003911|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_CURRENCY, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS CURRENCY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003912|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_UNIT_CURRENCY, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS UNIT_CURRENCY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003913|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_EXCHANGE_PRICE, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS EXCHANGE_PRICE   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003914|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_CREATED_BY, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS CREATED_BY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003915|         , TRIM(REGEXP_SUBSTR(P_LAST_UPDATED_BY, '(.*?)(\^|$)', 1, LEVEL, NULL, 1)) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY   --*[2026-09-07] POJ 빈값 보존형 split 적용{LF}|#
B|003916|    FROM   DUAL{LF}|#
B|003917|    CONNECT BY LEVEL <= v_max_cnt;{LF}|#
B|003918|    COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|003919|    --###############################################  GTT에 split후 insert (END){LF}|#
B|003920|    -- 20180806 KHSONG Division_id Null Validation 추가{LF}|#
B|003921|    BEGIN{LF}|#
B|003922|        SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|003923|        INTO   v_exists{LF}|#
B|003924|        FROM   DUAL{LF}|#
B|003925|        WHERE EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|003926|            SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|003927|            FROM   GT_F020_EXCHANGE_RATE R{LF}|#
B|003928|            WHERE  NOT EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|003929|                       SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|003930|                       FROM   SCM_MST_DIVISION D{LF}|#
B|003931|                       WHERE  D.DIVISION_ID = R.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|003932|                   ){LF}|#
B|003933|        );{LF}|#
B|003934|        -- Division 값이 존재하지 않는 레코드가 있음{LF}|#
B|003935|        v_log_type    := 'INVALID';{LF}|#
B|003936|        v_log_message := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|003937|        v_description := 'Division 값이 존재하지 않습니다.';{LF}|#
B|003938|        d_event_date  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|003939|        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, v_log_type, n_log_count, v_log_message, v_description, d_event_date); END;{LF}|#
B|003940|        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|003941|        SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: - Validation Check' AS MSG, v_description AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|003942|        RETURN;{LF}|#
B|003943|    EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|003944|        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN{LF}|#
B|003945|            NULL; -- 모든 Division이 유효함 → 정상 진행{LF}|#
B|003946|    END;{LF}|#
B|003947|    -- GTT 통계 수집{LF}|#
B|003948|    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|003949|        OWNNAME          => 'SCM_MGR',{LF}|#
B|003950|        TABNAME          => 'GT_F020_EXCHANGE_RATE',{LF}|#
B|003951|        ESTIMATE_PERCENT => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE,{LF}|#
B|003952|        METHOD_OPT       => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1',{LF}|#
B|003953|        NO_INVALIDATE    => FALSE{LF}|#
B|003954|    );{LF}|#
B|003955|    --===================================================================================== delete start{LF}|#
B|003956|    DELETE FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE{LF}|#
B|003957|    WHERE  EXISTS ({LF}|#
B|003958|               SELECT 1{LF}|#
B|003959|               FROM   GT_F020_EXCHANGE_RATE R{LF}|#
B|003960|               WHERE  R.ACTIVE_FLAG                       = 'D'{LF}|#
B|003961|               AND    DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.DIVISION_ID    = R.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|003962|               AND    DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.YYYYMMDD       = R.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003963|               AND    DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.CURRENCY       = R.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003964|               AND    DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE.UNIT_CURRENCY  = R.UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003965|           );{LF}|#
B|003966|    n_delete_count := SQL%ROWCOUNT;{LF}|#
B|003967|    --===================================================================================== delete end{LF}|#
B|003968|    --===================================================================================== update, insert start{LF}|#
B|003969|    MERGE INTO DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE A{LF}|#
B|003970|    USING ({LF}|#
B|003971|            SELECT DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|003972|                 , YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003973|                 , CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003974|                 , UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003975|                 , MAX(EXCHANGE_PRICE)  AS EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|003976|                 , MAX(CREATED_BY)      AS CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|003977|                 , MAX(LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY{LF}|#
B|003978|            FROM   GT_F020_EXCHANGE_RATE{LF}|#
B|003979|            WHERE  ACTIVE_FLAG <> 'D'   -- DELETE값이 아닌경우만{LF}|#
B|003980|            GROUP BY DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|003981|                   , YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003982|                   , CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003983|                   , UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003984|          ) B{LF}|#
B|003985|    ON ({LF}|#
B|003986|            A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|003987|        AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|003988|        AND A.CURRENCY      = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003989|        AND A.UNIT_CURRENCY = B.UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|003990|       ){LF}|#
B|003991|    WHEN MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|003992|        UPDATE SET{LF}|#
B|003993|            A.EXCHANGE_PRICE   = TO_NUMBER(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE),{LF}|#
B|003994|            A.CREATED_BY       = B.CREATED_BY,{LF}|#
B|003995|            A.CREATION_DATE    = SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|003996|            A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = B.LAST_UPDATED_BY,{LF}|#
B|003997|            A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = SYSDATE{LF}|#
B|003998|    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|003999|        INSERT ({LF}|#
B|004000|            DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|004001|            EXCHANGE_PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE,{LF}|#
B|004002|            LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE{LF}|#
B|004003|        ){LF}|#
B|004004|        VALUES ({LF}|#
B|004005|            B.DIVISION_ID, B.YYYYMMDD, B.CURRENCY, B.UNIT_CURRENCY,{LF}|#
B|004006|            TO_NUMBER(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE), B.CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|004007|            B.LAST_UPDATED_BY, SYSDATE{LF}|#
B|004008|        );{LF}|#
B|004009|    n_merge_count := SQL%ROWCOUNT;{LF}|#
B|004010|    --===================================================================================== update, insert end{LF}|#
B|004011|    COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|004012|    -- 종료 LOG{LF}|#
B|004013|    v_log_type    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|004014|    v_log_message := '########## END of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|004015|    v_description := 'delete_count: ' || TO_CHAR(n_delete_count) || ' / merge_count: ' || TO_CHAR(n_merge_count);{LF}|#
B|004016|    d_event_date  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|004017|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, v_log_type, n_log_count, v_log_message, v_description, d_event_date); END;{LF}|#
B|004018|    --============================================================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004019|    -- 결과코드 0 성공, -1 오류.{LF}|#
B|004020|    --============================================================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004021|    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR{LF}|#
B|004022|    SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, TO_CHAR(n_delete_count + n_merge_count) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;{LF}|#
B|004023|EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|004024|    WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|004025|        ROLLBACK;{LF}|#
B|004026|        V_LOG_TYPE_E    := 'ERROR';{LF}|#
B|004027|        V_LOG_MESSAGE_E := 'EXCEPTION';{LF}|#
B|004028|        V_DESCRIPTION_E := '';{LF}|#
B|004029|        V_SQL_CODE      := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);{LF}|#
B|004030|        V_SQL_ERRMSG    := SQLERRM;{LF}|#
B|004031|        V_PROC_INPUT    :={LF}|#
B|004032|            JSON_OBJECT({LF}|#
B|004033|                'P_ACTIVE_FLAG'     VALUE SUBSTR(P_ACTIVE_FLAG, 1, 500),{LF}|#
B|004034|                'P_DIVISION_ID'     VALUE SUBSTR(P_DIVISION_ID, 1, 500),{LF}|#
B|004035|                'P_YYYYMMDD'        VALUE SUBSTR(P_YYYYMMDD, 1, 500),{LF}|#
B|004036|                'P_CURRENCY'        VALUE SUBSTR(P_CURRENCY, 1, 500),{LF}|#

B|004038|                'P_EXCHANGE_PRICE'  VALUE SUBSTR(P_EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1, 500),{LF}|#
B|004039|                'P_CREATED_BY'      VALUE P_CREATED_BY,{LF}|#
B|004040|                'P_LAST_UPDATED_BY' VALUE P_LAST_UPDATED_BY{LF}|#
B|004041|            );{LF}|#
B|004042|        V_BACKTRACE     := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;{LF}|#
B|004043|        BEGIN{LF}|#
B|004044|            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH({LF}|#
B|004045|                $$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE_E, n_log_count, V_LOG_MESSAGE_E,{LF}|#
B|004046|                V_DESCRIPTION_E, SYSDATE, V_PROC_INPUT,{LF}|#
B|004047|                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE{LF}|#
B|004048|            );{LF}|#
B|004049|        END;{LF}|#
B|004050|        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,{LF}|#
B|004051|          '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||{LF}|#
B|004052|          ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||{LF}|#
B|004053|          ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||{LF}|#
B|004054|          ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT{LF}|#
B|004055|        );{LF}|#
B|004056|        RAISE;{LF}|#
B|004057|END P_DMF0020_SAVE_DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE_ALL;{LF}|#
B|004058|PROCEDURE P_DMF0020_SAVE_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_CONVERSION{LF}|#
B|004059|({LF}|#
B|004060|    P_DIVISION_ID     IN VARCHAR2,{LF}|#
B|004061|    O_RESULT_CURSOR   OUT SYS_REFCURSOR{LF}|#
B|004062|){LF}|#
B|004063|/*{LF}|#
B|004064| * Program ID   : DMF0020_SAVE_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_CONVERSION{LF}|#
B|004065| * Description  : SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE Data Conversion{LF}|#
B|004066| * Program History{LF}|#
B|004067| *==================================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004068| *   Date       In Charge      Description{LF}|#
B|004069| *==================================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004070| * 2017. 02. 22    KHSONG      Initial Release [PJ20170278] 전지본부 자동차 GSCM(DM/SCP) 구축 프로젝트{LF}|#
B|004071| * 20180720 KHSONG 주석처리 전체를 다시 계산함{LF}|#
B|004072| * 20190213 KHSONG [CSR ID:3921022] GSCM 판가 소수점 관리 개선 요청의 건 -> 소수점 3번째자리까지 반올림하여 저장{LF}|#
B|004073| * 2020/09/09     P.C.I{TAB}{TAB}[C20200909-000203] LOG 표준화 적용{LF}|#
B|004074| * 2021.05.13{TAB}P.C.I{TAB}{TAB}[PJ20209642R] Chempion 환율 적용{LF}|#
B|004075| * 2022.10.24{TAB}ROSIA27{TAB}{TAB}[C20221024-000510]DM UI 소스 정리 요청{LF}|#
B|004076| * 2025.03.25{TAB}KANG.H.J{TAB}[PJ2025-50017] 소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 - DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE 재생성{LF}|#
B|004077| * 2026.03.17  jcnam        [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발{LF}|#
B|004078| */{LF}|#
B|004079|-- Weekly Cutoff 시에 이 로직이 수행되어야 함.{LF}|#
B|004080|AS{LF}|#
B|004081|    -- 당주 월요일 - 7일 (MSSQL: DATEADD(DD, -7, dbo.FN_NEXT_DAY(GETDATE(), 'MON'))){LF}|#
B|004082|    v_cutoff_yyyymmdd  VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004083|    -- LOG 용 변수 설정{LF}|#
B|004084|    v_log_type         VARCHAR2(30 CHAR); -- START/END/ERROR/...{LF}|#
B|004085|    n_log_count        PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#
B|004086|    v_log_message      VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004087|    v_description      VARCHAR2(500 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004088|    d_event_date       DATE;{LF}|#
B|004089|    n_merge_count1     PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#
B|004090|    n_merge_count2     PLS_INTEGER := 0;{LF}|#
B|004091|    V_PROC_INPUT       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004092|    V_BACKTRACE        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004093|    V_SQL_CODE         VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004094|    V_SQL_ERRMSG       VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004095|    V_LOG_TYPE_E       VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004096|    V_LOG_MESSAGE_E    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004097|    V_DESCRIPTION_E    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);{LF}|#
B|004098|BEGIN{LF}|#
B|004099|    -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-07 기준 주 설명) (START){LF}|#
B|004100|--    -- 당주 월요일 - 7일 계산{LF}|#
B|004101|    -- 당주 월요일 계산{LF}|#
B|004102|    -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-07 기준 주 설명) (END){LF}|#
B|004103|    -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-07 기준 주) (START){LF}|#
B|004104|--    v_cutoff_yyyymmdd := TO_CHAR(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'IW') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD');{LF}|#
B|004105|    v_cutoff_yyyymmdd := TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD');{LF}|#
B|004106|    -- 2026.09.28 AJS 주석처리 (F-07 기준 주) (END){LF}|#
B|004107|    -- 시작 LOG  (START){LF}|#
B|004108|    v_log_type    := 'START';{LF}|#
B|004109|    v_log_message := '########## Start of procedure ##########';{LF}|#
B|004110|    v_description := 'DIVISION_ID : ' || P_DIVISION_ID;{LF}|#
B|004111|    d_event_date  := SYSDATE;{LF}|#
B|004112|    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, v_log_type, n_log_count, v_log_message, v_description, d_event_date); END;{LF}|#
B|004113|    -- GTT TRUNCATE{LF}|#
B|004114|    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_F020_FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP2;{LF}|#
B|004115|    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_F020_CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP2;{LF}|#
B|004116|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004117|    -- STEP 1: #FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP1 → WITH절, #FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP2 → GTT INSERT{LF}|#
B|004118|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004119|    BEGIN{LF}|#
B|004120|        EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION ENABLE PARALLEL DML';{LF}|#
B|004121|    EXCEPTION{LF}|#
B|004122|        WHEN OTHERS THEN{LF}|#
B|004123|            IF SQLCODE != -12841 THEN{LF}|#
B|004124|                RAISE;{LF}|#
B|004125|            END IF;{LF}|#
B|004126|    END;{LF}|#
B|004127|    INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO SCM_MGR.GT_F020_FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP2{LF}|#
B|004128|    (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY){LF}|#
B|004129|    WITH FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP1 AS ({LF}|#
B|004130|        SELECT /*+ FULL(F) PARALLEL(F,4) */{LF}|#
B|004131|               DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|004132|        FROM   SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE F{LF}|#
B|004133|        WHERE  DIVISION_ID = P_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004134|        AND    YYYYMMDD   >= v_cutoff_yyyymmdd  -- 당주 월요일이후 데이터만 대상으로함{LF}|#
B|004135|        AND    MEASURE     = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|004136|    ){LF}|#
B|004137|    SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, ROUND(PRICE, 3) AS PRICE, CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|004138|    FROM ({LF}|#
B|004139|        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004140|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004141|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN 'USD'{LF}|#
B|004142|                    ELSE 'KRW' END                                        AS MEASURE{LF}|#
B|004143|             , DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|004144|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN PRICE{LF}|#
B|004145|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN PRICE{LF}|#
B|004146|                    ELSE PRICE * NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1) END             AS PRICE{LF}|#
B|004147|             , A.CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|004148|        FROM   FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP1 A{LF}|#
B|004149|        LEFT OUTER JOIN DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE B{LF}|#
B|004150|            ON  A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004151|            AND SUBSTR(A.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) || '01' = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|004152|            AND B.CURRENCY      = 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004153|            AND A.CURRENCY      = B.UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004154|        UNION ALL{LF}|#
B|004155|        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004156|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN 'USD'{LF}|#
B|004157|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004158|                    ELSE 'USD' END                                        AS MEASURE{LF}|#
B|004159|             , DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|004160|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN NVL(PRICE / NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1), 0){LF}|#
B|004161|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN NVL(PRICE * NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1), 0){LF}|#
B|004162|                    ELSE NVL({LF}|#
B|004163|                            (A.PRICE * NVL({LF}|#
B|004164|                                (SELECT DMER.EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|004165|                                 FROM   DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE DMER{LF}|#
B|004166|                                 WHERE  DMER.DIVISION_ID   = A.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004167|                                 AND    DMER.YYYYMMDD      = SUBSTR(A.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) || '01'{LF}|#
B|004168|                                 AND    DMER.UNIT_CURRENCY  = A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004169|                                 AND    DMER.CURRENCY       = 'KRW'), 1)){LF}|#
B|004170|                            / NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1), 0){LF}|#
B|004171|               END                                                        AS PRICE{LF}|#
B|004172|             , A.CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|004173|        FROM   FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP1 A{LF}|#
B|004174|        LEFT OUTER JOIN DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE B{LF}|#
B|004175|            ON  A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004176|            AND SUBSTR(A.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) || '01' = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|004177|            AND B.CURRENCY       = 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004178|            AND B.UNIT_CURRENCY  = 'USD'{LF}|#
B|004179|    ) C{LF}|#
B|004180|    WHERE PRICE > 0;{LF}|#
B|004181|    COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|004182|    --EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION DISABLE PARALLEL DML';{LF}|#
B|004183|    -- GTT 통계 수집{LF}|#
B|004184|    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|004185|        OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_F020_FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP2',{LF}|#
B|004186|        ESTIMATE_PERCENT => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE,{LF}|#
B|004187|        METHOD_OPT => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE{LF}|#
B|004188|    );{LF}|#
B|004189|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004190|    -- STEP 2: MERGE INTO SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|004191|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004192|    MERGE INTO SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE A{LF}|#
B|004193|    USING GT_F020_FUTURE_PRICE_TEMP2 B{LF}|#
B|004194|    ON (    A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004195|        AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = B.PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|004196|        AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = B.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|004197|        AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|004198|        AND A.ITEM_ID       = B.ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|004199|        AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|004200|        AND A.CURRENCY      = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004201|        AND A.MEASURE       = B.MEASURE{LF}|#
B|004202|        AND A.DOM_EXP       = B.DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|004203|        AND A.FCST_TYPE     = B.FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|004204|        AND A.INCOTERMS     = B.INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|004205|       ){LF}|#
B|004206|    WHEN MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|004207|        UPDATE SET A.PRICE            = B.PRICE,{LF}|#
B|004208|                   A.CREATION_DATE    = SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|004209|                   A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = 'SYSTEM',{LF}|#
B|004210|                   A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = SYSDATE{LF}|#
B|004211|    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|004212|        INSERT (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE,{LF}|#
B|004213|                DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE,{LF}|#
B|004214|                ATTRIBUTE1, ATTRIBUTE2, ATTRIBUTE3, ATTRIBUTE4, ATTRIBUTE5, ATTRIBUTE6, ATTRIBUTE7, ATTRIBUTE8, ATTRIBUTE9, ATTRIBUTE10){LF}|#
B|004215|        VALUES (NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.DIVISION_ID),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.PROJECT_CODE),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.SCM_SITE_ID),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.PLANT_SITE_ID),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.ITEM_ID),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.YYYYMMDD),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.CURRENCY),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.MEASURE),''), '/'),{LF}|#
B|004216|                NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.DOM_EXP),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.FCST_TYPE),''), '/'), NVL(NULLIF(RTRIM(B.INCOTERMS),''), '/'), B.PRICE, B.CREATED_BY, SYSDATE, 'SYSTEM', SYSDATE,{LF}|#
B|004217|                NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);{LF}|#
B|004218|    n_merge_count1 := SQL%ROWCOUNT;{LF}|#
B|004219|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004220|    -- STEP 3: [PJ2025-50017] #CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP1 → WITH절, TEMP2 → GTT INSERT{LF}|#
B|004221|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004222|    --EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION ENABLE PARALLEL DML';{LF}|#
B|004223|    INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO SCM_MGR.GT_F020_CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP2{LF}|#
B|004224|    (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE){LF}|#
B|004225|    WITH CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP1 AS ({LF}|#
B|004226|        SELECT /*+ FULL(F) PARALLEL(F,4) */{LF}|#
B|004227|               DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|004228|        FROM   DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE F{LF}|#
B|004229|        WHERE  DIVISION_ID = P_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004230|        AND    YYYYMMDD   >= v_cutoff_yyyymmdd  -- 당주 월요일이후 데이터만 대상으로함{LF}|#
B|004231|        AND    MEASURE     = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|004232|    ){LF}|#
B|004233|    SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, ROUND(PRICE, 3) AS PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|004234|    FROM ({LF}|#
B|004235|        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004236|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004237|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN 'USD'{LF}|#
B|004238|                    ELSE 'KRW' END                                        AS MEASURE{LF}|#
B|004239|             , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|004240|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN A.PRICE{LF}|#
B|004241|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN A.PRICE{LF}|#
B|004242|                    ELSE A.PRICE * NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1) END           AS PRICE{LF}|#
B|004243|             , A.CREATED_BY, A.CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|004244|        FROM   CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP1 A{LF}|#
B|004245|        LEFT OUTER JOIN DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE B{LF}|#
B|004246|            ON  A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004247|            AND SUBSTR(A.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) || '01' = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|004248|            AND B.CURRENCY      = 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004249|            AND A.CURRENCY      = B.UNIT_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004250|        UNION ALL{LF}|#
B|004251|        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004252|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN 'USD'{LF}|#
B|004253|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004254|                    ELSE 'USD' END                                        AS MEASURE{LF}|#
B|004255|             , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|004256|             , CASE WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'KRW' THEN NVL(A.PRICE / NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1), 0){LF}|#
B|004257|                    WHEN A.CURRENCY = 'USD' THEN NVL(A.PRICE * NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1), 0){LF}|#
B|004258|                    ELSE NVL({LF}|#
B|004259|                            (A.PRICE * NVL({LF}|#
B|004260|                                (SELECT DMER.EXCHANGE_PRICE{LF}|#
B|004261|                                 FROM   DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE DMER{LF}|#
B|004262|                                 WHERE  DMER.DIVISION_ID   = A.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004263|                                 AND    DMER.YYYYMMDD      = SUBSTR(A.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) || '01'{LF}|#
B|004264|                                 AND    DMER.UNIT_CURRENCY  = A.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004265|                                 AND    DMER.CURRENCY       = 'KRW'), 1)){LF}|#
B|004266|                            / NVL(B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 1), 0){LF}|#
B|004267|               END                                                        AS PRICE{LF}|#
B|004268|             , A.CREATED_BY, A.CREATION_DATE{LF}|#
B|004269|        FROM   CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP1 A{LF}|#
B|004270|        LEFT OUTER JOIN DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE B{LF}|#
B|004271|            ON  A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|004272|            AND SUBSTR(A.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) || '01' = B.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|004273|            AND B.CURRENCY       = 'KRW'{LF}|#
B|004274|            AND B.UNIT_CURRENCY  = 'USD'{LF}|#
B|004275|    ) C{LF}|#
B|004276|    WHERE PRICE > 0;{LF}|#
B|004277|    COMMIT;{LF}|#
B|004278|    --EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION DISABLE PARALLEL DML';{LF}|#
B|004279|    -- GTT 통계 수집{LF}|#
B|004280|    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS({LF}|#
B|004281|        OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_F020_CFM_SELLING_PRICE_TEMP2',{LF}|#
B|004282|        ESTIMATE_PERCENT => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE,{LF}|#
B|004283|        METHOD_OPT => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE{LF}|#
B|004284|    );{LF}|#
B|004285|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004286|    -- STEP 4: [PJ2025-50017] MERGE INTO DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE{LF}|#
B|004287|    -- ====================================================================================={LF}|#
B|004288|    MERGE INTO DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE A{LF}|#

B|004298|        AND A.CURRENCY         = B.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|004299|        AND A.MEASURE          = B.MEASURE{LF}|#
B|004300|        AND A.DOM_EXP          = B.DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|004301|        AND A.FCST_TYPE        = B.FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|004302|        AND A.INCOTERMS        = B.INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|004303|       ){LF}|#
B|004304|    WHEN MATCHED THEN{LF}|#
B|004305|        UPDATE SET A.PRICE            = B.PRICE,{LF}|#
B|004306|                   A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = 'EXCHANGE_RATE',{LF}|#

B|004308|    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN{LF}|#

B|006561|            ,V_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|006562|            ,V_PRICE{LF}|#
B|006563|            ,V_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|006564|        ){LF}|#
B|006565|        SELECT{LF}|#
B|006566|             V_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|006567|            ,V_PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|006568|            ,V_SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006569|            ,V_ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|006570|            ,V_DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|006571|            ,V_FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|006572|            ,V_INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|006573|            ,V_MEASURE{LF}|#
B|006574|            ,V_PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006575|            ,V_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|006576|            ,V_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|006577|            ,V_PRICE{LF}|#
B|006578|            ,V_CREATED_BY{LF}|#
B|006579|        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_FCSV_RESULTS_MST{LF}|#
B|006580|        WHERE  V_ACTIVE_FLAG <> 'D';   -- DELETE값이 아닌경우만{LF}|#
B|006581|        -- Procedure 호출 #results 테이블의 데이터를 전달하여 처리함{LF}|#
B|006582|        P_PR_DM_ANL_SALES_PLAN_AMT_CREATE_LOCAL_KRW_SINGLE;{LF}|#
B|006583|        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------  20180510 SKH ADD END{LF}|#
B|006584|    END;{LF}|#
B|006585|    --===================================================================================== update, insert end{LF}|#
B|006586|    --[PJ20200138] 입력된 Price가 주변 주차와 Range Rate 가 초과되어 차이나는 데이터 Return{LF}|#
B|006587|    OPEN O_RC1 FOR{LF}|#
B|006588|    WITH W_MIN_MAX_DATE (ROWNUMVAL, YYYYMMDD, MIN_YYYYMMDD, MAX_YYYYMMDD) AS{LF}|#
B|006589|    ({LF}|#
B|006590|        SELECT{LF}|#
B|006591|             R.V_ROWNUM AS ROWNUMVAL{LF}|#
B|006592|            ,R.V_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|006593|            ,(SELECT NVL(MAX(S_MIN.YYYYMMDD), ''){LF}|#
B|006594|              FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE S_MIN{LF}|#
B|006595|              WHERE  S_MIN.DIVISION_ID  = R.V_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|006596|              AND    S_MIN.PROJECT_CODE = R.V_PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|006597|              AND    S_MIN.SCM_SITE_ID  = R.V_SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006598|              AND    S_MIN.PLANT_SITE_ID= R.V_PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006599|              AND    S_MIN.ITEM_ID      = R.V_ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|006600|              AND    S_MIN.CURRENCY     = R.V_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|006601|              AND    S_MIN.MEASURE      = R.V_MEASURE{LF}|#
B|006602|              AND    S_MIN.DOM_EXP      = R.V_DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|006603|              AND    S_MIN.FCST_TYPE    = R.V_FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|006604|              AND    S_MIN.INCOTERMS    = R.V_INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|006605|              AND    S_MIN.MEASURE      = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|006606|              AND    S_MIN.YYYYMMDD     < R.V_YYYYMMDD) AS MIN_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|006607|            ,(SELECT NVL(MIN(S_MAX.YYYYMMDD), ''){LF}|#
B|006608|              FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE S_MAX{LF}|#
B|006609|              WHERE  S_MAX.DIVISION_ID  = R.V_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|006610|              AND    S_MAX.PROJECT_CODE = R.V_PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|006611|              AND    S_MAX.SCM_SITE_ID  = R.V_SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006612|              AND    S_MAX.PLANT_SITE_ID= R.V_PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006613|              AND    S_MAX.ITEM_ID      = R.V_ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|006614|              AND    S_MAX.CURRENCY     = R.V_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|006615|              AND    S_MAX.MEASURE      = R.V_MEASURE{LF}|#
B|006616|              AND    S_MAX.DOM_EXP      = R.V_DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|006617|              AND    S_MAX.FCST_TYPE    = R.V_FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|006618|              AND    S_MAX.INCOTERMS    = R.V_INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|006619|              AND    S_MAX.MEASURE      = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|006620|              AND    S_MAX.YYYYMMDD     > R.V_YYYYMMDD) AS MAX_YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|006621|        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_FCSV_RESULTS_MST R{LF}|#
B|006622|        WHERE  1 = 1{LF}|#
B|006623|        AND    R.V_MEASURE = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|006624|        AND    R.V_ACTIVE_FLAG <> 'D'   -- 2022.07.22{LF}|#
B|006625|    ){LF}|#
B|006626|    , W_RETURN AS{LF}|#
B|006627|    ({LF}|#
B|006628|        SELECT{LF}|#
B|006629|             R.V_ROWNUM{LF}|#
B|006630|            ,(CASE WHEN S.DIVISION_ID = '40' THEN S.PROJECT_CODE ELSE C.END_CUSTOMER END) AS END_CUSTOMER{LF}|#
B|006631|            ,S.PROJECT_CODE, S.SCM_SITE_ID, S.ITEM_ID, S.INCOTERMS, S.FCST_TYPE, S.CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|006632|            ,S.DIVISION_ID, S.PLANT_SITE_ID, S.MEASURE, S.DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|006633|            ,R.V_YYYYMMDD AS CH_YYYYMMDD, CAST(R.V_PRICE AS NUMBER(13,3)) AS CH_PRICE{LF}|#
B|006634|            ,S.YYYYMMDD   AS ST_YYYYMMDD, CAST(S.PRICE   AS NUMBER(13,3)) AS ST_PRICE{LF}|#
B|006635|            ,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY R.V_ROWNUM, R.V_YYYYMMDD ORDER BY R.V_ROWNUM, R.V_YYYYMMDD) AS REP_ROWNUM{LF}|#
B|006636|        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_FCSV_RESULTS_MST R{LF}|#
B|006637|        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE S{LF}|#
B|006638|            ON  S.DIVISION_ID  = R.V_DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|006639|            AND S.PROJECT_CODE = R.V_PROJECT_CODE{LF}|#
B|006640|            AND S.SCM_SITE_ID  = R.V_SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006641|            AND S.PLANT_SITE_ID= R.V_PLANT_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006642|            AND S.ITEM_ID      = R.V_ITEM_ID{LF}|#
B|006643|            AND S.CURRENCY     = R.V_CURRENCY{LF}|#
B|006644|            AND S.MEASURE      = R.V_MEASURE{LF}|#
B|006645|            AND S.DOM_EXP      = R.V_DOM_EXP{LF}|#
B|006646|            AND S.FCST_TYPE    = R.V_FCST_TYPE{LF}|#
B|006647|            AND S.INCOTERMS    = R.V_INCOTERMS{LF}|#
B|006648|        INNER JOIN W_MIN_MAX_DATE W{LF}|#
B|006649|            ON  R.V_ROWNUM     = W.ROWNUMVAL{LF}|#
B|006650|            AND R.V_YYYYMMDD   = W.YYYYMMDD{LF}|#
B|006651|        LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE C{LF}|#
B|006652|            ON  S.DIVISION_ID = C.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|006653|            AND S.SCM_SITE_ID = C.SCM_SITE_ID{LF}|#
B|006654|            AND C.SITE_TYPE   = 'SALES'{LF}|#
B|006655|        JOIN{LF}|#
B|006656|        ({LF}|#
B|006657|            SELECT SEGMENT1 AS DIVISION_ID, SEGMENT2 AS RANGE_VAL{LF}|#
B|006658|            FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL{LF}|#
B|006659|            WHERE  VALID_YN    = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|006660|            AND    LOOKUP_TYPE = 'SALES_PRICE_RANGE_RATE'{LF}|#
B|006661|        ) L{LF}|#
B|006662|            ON  S.DIVISION_ID = L.DIVISION_ID{LF}|#
B|006663|        WHERE  S.MEASURE  = 'LOC'{LF}|#
B|006664|        AND    C.VALID_YN = 'Y'{LF}|#
B|006665|        AND    (S.PRICE * L.RANGE_VAL) < ABS(S.PRICE - R.V_PRICE){LF}|#
B|006666|        AND    S.YYYYMMDD IN (W.MIN_YYYYMMDD, W.MAX_YYYYMMDD){LF}|#
B|006667|    ){LF}|#
B|006668|    SELECT *{LF}|#
B|006669|    FROM   W_RETURN{LF}|#
B|006670|    WHERE  REP_ROWNUM = 1{LF}|#
B|006671|    ORDER BY 1, 2, 3, 8, 9;{LF}|#
B|006672|    -- 종료 LOG{LF}|#
B|006673|    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';{LF}|#
B|006674|    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure @@@##########';{LF}|#

B|007177|{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}   END IF;{LF}|#

B|007179|{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}     FROM GT_DMF0020_RESULTS_MST R{LF}|#
B|007180|{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}    WHERE ( SELECT count(1) FROM SCM_MST_ITEM_SN WHERE ITEM_ID = R.v_ITEM_ID ) = 0;{LF}|#

B|007185|{TAB}{TAB}{TAB}   END IF;{LF}|#
